# bgfx / RenderDragon map (1.26.62.01)

## 0. What fully reverse engineering means here

**Full reverse engineering** of the rendering stack means recovering not just where a function lives (an RVA) but what it does, what data it touches, and how the pieces compose into a frame. An RVA is only a starting point: it tells you *where* a function is, not *what* it does. Surface-level RVAs + hooks let you intercept a call; full RE lets you read, edit, reorder or replace any part of the pipeline because you know the struct layouts, control flow, frame graph, and actor-to-draw route.

The renderer is a stack, not bare bgfx (see section 0 "What bgfx is here" below):

```
game (ActorRenderDispatcher, LevelRenderer, ParticleEngine, tessellator ...)
  -> mce::framebuilder::FrameBuilder::insert<TDescription>()   typed per-thing descriptions
  -> BgfxFrameBuilder::_insert(group variant)                  extracts into dragon::frameobject components (entt registry)
  -> BgfxFrameBuilder::endFrame                                builds Frame<...> + dragon framegraph
  -> dragon::platform::BgfxContext::endFrame                   "Generate and encode frame graph"
  -> dragon::tasks::GraphicsTasks (render thread)              "Execute rendering tasks and frame"
  -> bgfx::Context (Mojang fork, global)                       handle allocators, uniforms, textures, views, Frame
  -> RendererContextD3D12::submit (0xe82e230) [I]             walks the Frame and records D3D12
  -> D3D12
```

### Six capabilities unlocked by full RE

1. **Data structures** — recover every struct layout (Frame, View, RenderDraw header, RenderBind record, per-handle resource tables, handle allocators) so you can read any field at runtime.
2. **Control flow** — map each function's prologue, callees and branches so you know the real argument list and call order (a detour that drops an argument crashes).
3. **Frame graph** — decode the dragon Declarator/pass declarations and the 0x80-byte task list so you can enable, disable or reorder whole passes by name hash.
4. **Actor-to-draw route** — trace game entity -> description -> extractor -> render object -> draw record so a draw can be mapped back to an actor (identity is NOT in the batch instance; it comes from the registry/components or from bodyActor S+0xC8).
5. **Pass add/remove/reorder** — edit the declared pass list or the task list entries (swap std::function impl pointers) without breaking the graph.
6. **Edit draws at submit** — intercept Encoder::submit or the executor/batcher to veto, rewrite state, rebind textures, or skip individual draws/batches.

### Full-RE plan: layers L1-L18

Goals and names taken verbatim from docs/RENDER_REVERSAL_PLAN.md:

| Layer | Name | Goal |
|---|---|---|
| L1 | Frame, submit and the bgfx core | Reverse bgfx::Context live layout and its global, Frame / RenderItem / RenderBind / View / ViewRemap structs and strides, the encoder, setViewRect/Clear/Transform entry points, uniform registry and predefined uniforms, handle allocators (paged guarded arrays), the API thread to render thread handoff, async init, shutdown, dynamic texture handles, RT/TLAS slots of the fork. Output: Context/Frame/RenderItem/View/Encoder/Handles SDK headers, FrameObserver hook at submit. |
| L2 | RendererContextD3D12 and the D3D12 backend | All 65 vtable slots (names, args), submit body (view loop, sort, draw dispatch, state-to-PSO lookup), PSO cache and key layout, root signature, descriptor heaps/allocation, command queue wrapper (+0xCC9268), resource creation, state tracking, fences, swapchain/present, device-lost/TDR, GPU timers, shader loading, RTX/TLAS build, upload rings. Output: d3d12/*.h headers, offsets in OffsetProvider.h. |
| L3 | RenderDragon: platform, tasks, scheduler, frame graph encoding | dragon::platform::BgfxContext::endFrame ("Generate and encode frame graph"), GraphicsTasks (frame lambda, _render, waitForPreviousFrame), the task scheduler, task record layout, DragonFrameResources, IntraFrameAllocator, the Declarator, module/pass declaration system, how a frame graph compiles to bgfx calls. Output: dragon/*.h headers; hook to enumerate/enable/disable passes by name. |
| L4 | FrameBuilder layer (mce::framebuilder) | BgfxFrameBuilder (114 vtable slots; names for all; fields +0x20/+0x28/+0x358), the six group _insert slots (Editor 0x358, Effect 0x360, UI 0x368, Sky 0x370, Item 0x378, Light 0x380), every description type and tag order, layout of each description, beginFrame/endFrame, TDR handling. Output: one header per description, typed hooks per group. |
| L5 | Extractors and the registry | The Item visitor (0x6646980) jump table (0xf2bf2d0, 11 entries) and each extractor behind it (BatchActor 0x66d9af0, skinned mesh inline, chunk geometry, crack, nametag, block entity ...); the entt registry and every render component; how components become extractAndDrawWithExclusion lists; where per-actor identity, world transform and material come from. Output: component headers, ExtractionObserver, identity route for per-actor control. |
| L6 | Frame-graph renderers and pass classes | VibrantVisualsFrameRenderer (builder 0x55f1450), FancyFrameRenderer, RTXMinecraftFrameRenderer, legacy path; complete pass list per renderer with names, inputs/outputs, order and enable conditions. Output: pass registry (name -> RVA, in/out resources); disable/reorder/add passes. |
| L7 | Materials and shaders | .material.bin loader, MaterialPtr and material manager, variants/permutations, how a draw picks one, render-state/blend/depth/stencil setup, uniform buffers and binding lists, shader compile/cache, resource-pack custom material path. Output: Material*.h headers; hook to swap a draw's material/program. |
| L8 | Textures, atlases and resources | Texture group and ClientTexture/BedrockTexture/TexturePtr, how resource packs become GPU textures, atlases, mipmaps/compression, async upload, handle lifetime, render targets and formats, dynamic-texture-handle mechanism, pack reload path. |
| L9 | Geometry production: chunks, meshing, buffers | RenderChunkCoordinator (_launchVisibilityRebuild 0x7923d50), LevelBuilder::scheduleChunkBuild (0x533c910), the mesher (tessellator, palette, per-face culling, AO), chunk mesh storage, VB/IB pools and upload, chunk occlusion/visibility, LOD/draw distance, sub-chunk draw lists. |
| L10 | Level renderer, camera, culling and scene setup | LevelRenderer / LevelRendererPlayer (camera pos, projection), frustum extraction and culling, view/projection/jitter, viewport and render-resolution selection, dynamic resolution, FOV and screen-space transforms, per-frame constant data, weather/sky/fog parameters. |
| L11 | Lighting, shadows and environment | Shadow cascade setup and caster lists, light volumes and culling, ambient/sun/moon parameters, SSAO/SSR parameters, fog/volumetric parameters, probes/IBL, RT lighting. |
| L12 | Actor rendering above the framebuilder | ActorRenderDispatcher and actor renderers (data-driven body 0x6ebb510, player renderer 0x575efc0, 0x6b84e60), Molang evaluation for render, geometry/skeleton/bone matrices and upload (PrevBones), animation controllers, skins/capes, held-item and armor rendering, hands, nametags, shadows blob. |
| L13 | Particles, weather, sky and clouds | Particle engine to Particle descriptions and draw (billboards, instancing), weather (rain/snow), sky (celestial, stars, clouds, cubemap), end sky. |
| L14 | UI rendering | ScreenRenderer/MinecraftUIRenderContext, Ore UI/cohtml integration, text/font rendering, nine-slice, UI group descriptions, how UI draws into the frame, HUD/screen composition, UI passes, menu panorama path. |
| L15 | Post-processing, upscaling, HDR | TAA and history, DLSS (wrapper slots 60/64), FSR1 (EASU), TAAU, dynamic resolution, bloom, tonemap, color grading, motion vectors (VV only), checkerboard. |
| L16 | Ray tracing (RTX) | The Direct3D 12 RTX context, TLAS/BLAS build and update, RT shaders and passes, denoiser, RT slots in the bgfx fork. Lowest priority unless RTX hardware available. |
| L17 | Platform glue and threading | Swapchain/DXGI/present and resize, GDK integration, GPU timers and memory budget, device lost/TDR, exact thread model (game thread, dragon task threads, bgfx render thread), frame pacing/vsync, async init/shutdown ordering, graphics options plumbing. |
| L18 | Whole-frame replacement | Drive the frame ourselves, from a frame-level takeover of submit to replacing a renderer's pass list to a custom renderer fed by the extractors. Builds the optimization module (frame cost, culling, batching, own upscale). |

### RVAs as starting point: what you derive from an RVA

An RVA alone is not a mapping. From a single RVA you derive:

- **Prologue** -> real argument list (which register is `this`, whether an argument is dropped, stack layout). A detour that changes the signature crashes.
- **Callees** (via `fsum.py`) -> what it calls, indirect-call offsets, strings; the call graph reveals role.
- **Strings** -> what it does (error messages, format strings, pass/material names, uniform names).
- **Vtable slots** -> interface membership and virtual dispatch (slot index = interface position; overriding slot = subclass specialization).
- **Field accesses** (`this+K`) -> struct layout; repeated `this+0x19F58` etc. identifies object roles.
- **Loops** (`imul` stride, `lea` indexed addressing) -> arrays/tables and element strides (e.g. `idx<<7` = 0x80-byte draw records, `viewId*0x140` = 0x140-byte view records).
- **Branches** (`cmp`/`je` chains, jump tables) -> enums/switches and dispatch tags (Item visitor tag byte -> 11-way jump table).

Confidence tags used throughout this doc: **[V]** verified in disassembly or live (Cheat Engine read-only / hardware breakpoints), **[I]** inferred from structure or order, **[?]** open / not confirmed.

### Concrete example: mapping submit RVA 0xe82e230

Slot 50 of the RendererContextD3D12 vtable (base 0xF4ED2D0; RTX subclass 0xF4EEE80 overrides with a 0x381-byte wrapper at 0xe83d440 that pre-does TLAS/BLAS then calls base). submit is 0x4D91 bytes [I from size/shape]. Pseudocode of what the body does, annotated with the live-verified Frame layout offsets from section 6e [V = confirmed in a live Frame dump]:

```
submit(RendererContext* this, Frame* frame, r8, r9):   // rcx = context, rdx = Frame*
    // prologue + timers (QPC x2)
    // frame begin / state reset: 0xe81d660
    // descriptor/upload ring begin: 0xe8360f0 x3
    // per-view setup: 0xe818490 x3
    // clear / RT transitions: 0xe820910

    // ---- per-draw consumer ----
    // Draw index allocated by CAS on frame+0xDC57E8 (cap 0xFFFF = maxDrawCalls 65535) [I]
    // Per-view block table: [frame+0xDC57E0][viewSlot]  [I]
    // Backbuffer size: frame+0xDC5808 {u32 width, u32 height} = 1920x1137 [V]
    // Frame counter: frame+0xE746D8 u32 (rises by 1 per frame) [V]
    // Renderer state: this+0x19F88 int (<5 = skip) [I]
    // Resource tables: this+0x19F58 ptr -> guarded paged heap arrays [I]
    // Per-handle records: this+0x93A178 array of 0x78-byte records [I]

    // draw records: frame+0xB4000, stride 0x80 [V stride; base corrected to 0xB4700 in 6am [V]]
    for each draw record d at frame+0xB4000 + idx*0x80:
        u32 program   = d[+0x38]        [I]
        u32 startIdx  = d[+0x40]        [I]
        u32 indexCnt  = d[+0x44]        [I]   (0xFFFFFFFF = computed = buffer size / index stride [V])
        u16 ibHandle  = d[+0x5A]        [V]   (<<5 indexes the 0x20-stride index buffer table at context+0x93A178)
        u16 vbHandle  = d[+0x76]        [V]   (0xFFFF = none; <<7 indexes 0x80-stride vertex buffer record array)
        // state word d[+0x20] u64 -> PSO key mask 0x0707007FFFFFF0FF [V]
        // bind slots: d[+0x63] bitmask of 8 populated slots; slot k at d+k*8 (+4 u16 handle, +6 u16 flags) [V]
        // issue draw via command-list wrapper ([wrapper+0x30] object, vtable +0x158 draw / +0x1d8 set-vertex-buffers / +0xd0 barrier) [V]

    // tail: stats, present prep: 0xe81d850
```

Key derived facts [V/I]:
- `rdx` alternates between two Frame buffers 0xE749C0 bytes apart (double-buffered render/submit) [V].
- submit, endFrame (0x66232b0) and BgfxContext::endFrame (0xc270460) fire 1:1:1 per frame [V].
- The 0x80-byte header is the encoder-side copy built by Encoder::submit (0xbc5fb40) [V]; the executor 0xe821120 reads it and the 0x540-byte RenderDraw record at [frame+0x8B4700]+idx*0x540 [V].

This is the highest-value hook point in the whole stack: per-draw statistics, state dedup, instancing merge, and (with full RE) editing any field above before the D3D12 draw is emitted. [I]

Target: `levilauncher.exe\versions\1.26.62.01\Minecraft.Windows.exe`. All RVAs below are for that build unless marked. Static analysis only (Cheat Engine bridge was not reachable): every item is tagged **[V]** verified in disassembly/strings, **[I]** inferred (structure/LeviLamina order), **[?]** open.

Tools: `tools/sigs/xref.py` (existing), `tools/rdis`, and three new helpers in `tools/sigs/`:
- `srcloc.py "<__FUNCSIG__ substring>"` - finds the function that owns a compiler-emitted function-name string (record = pointer-slot - 8, then `lea` refs). This is how all `_insert`, `endFrame`, `GraphicsTasks::*` functions below were located with no RTTI.
- `vtdump.py <vtable rva> <n>` - slot -> function, size, strings.
- `fsum.py <rva>...` - callees, indirect-call offsets, strings.

## 0. What "bgfx" is here

LeviLamina's renderer headers (`src-client/mc/deps/minecraft_renderer`, `src/mc/external/bgfx`) are **names only** (empty structs) except `FrameBuilder.h` (virtual list, description variants), `ActorRenderDispatcher.h`, `ActorRenderData.h`, `BaseActorRenderContext.h`, `MinecraftGraphics.h`. The binary has no game RTTI (only lambda typeinfo), so layouts come from disassembly.

The renderer is a stack, not a bare bgfx:

```
game (ActorRenderDispatcher, LevelRenderer, ParticleEngine, tessellator ...)
  -> mce::framebuilder::FrameBuilder::insert<TDescription>()        typed per-thing descriptions
  -> BgfxFrameBuilder::_insert(group variant)                        extracts into dragon::frameobject components (entt registry)
  -> BgfxFrameBuilder::endFrame                                      builds Frame<...> + dragon framegraph (Vibrant Visuals / Fancy / RTX / legacy)
  -> dragon::platform::BgfxContext::endFrame                         "Generate and encode frame graph"
  -> dragon::tasks::GraphicsTasks (render thread)                    "GraphicsTasks - Execute rendering tasks and frame"
  -> bgfx::Context (Mojang fork, global)                             handle allocators, uniforms, textures, views, Frame
  -> RendererContextD3D12 (65-slot RendererContextI vtable)          ::submit walks the Frame and records D3D12
```
Mojang's fork: paged guarded handle arrays ("accessing a guarded paged heap backed array out of bounds"), async init (`initAsync`, "InitBegin has not been called"), dynamic texture handles, ray tracing slots (TLAS/BLAS), `BGFX_DEBUG_TRACE_ENABLED`, GPU timers.

## 2. RendererContextD3D12 [V/I]

- vtable at `.rdata` 0xf4 region: slot0 `0xe83c630` (dtor), 65 slots. `getRendererName` returns "Direct3D 12" (`0xe80f610`); D3D11 `0xe800de0`, "Direct3D 12 RTX" `0xe83d200` are sibling contexts.
- slot 58 (+0x1D0) `0xe807ff0` init: loads `d3d12.dll`, `D3D12CreateDevice`, `D3D12SerializeRootSignature`, `D3D12EnableExperimentalFeatures`.
- slot 50 (+0x190) **`0xe82e230`, size 0x4D91 = `submit(Frame*, ...)`** [I from size/shape: `rdx` = Frame*, uses Frame offsets `0xDC57F0..0xDC5808`, `0xE746D8`; this+`0x19F58` resource tables, +`0x238`, +`0x410`]. This is the per-draw consumer: the place to count/skip/merge draws.
- Likely order (bgfx `RendererContextI`, verified only where noted): 7 `0xe8102b0` (big, "Invalid Shader / Failed to create resource"), 10 createIndexBuffer, 14 createVertexBuffer, 16/17 dynamic index create/update, 19/20 dynamic vertex create/update, 22 `0xe8137b0` createShader ("Unknown shader format" [V]), 23 destroyShader, 24 createProgram, 26 createTexture, 28 updateTexture, 30 readTexture, 31 resizeTexture, 34 destroyTexture, 35/36 createFrameBuffer, 37 destroyFrameBuffer, 39+ uniforms. No-op destroys share `0xe812b90`/`0xe812e80`/`0x768f0`. [I]

## 3. FrameBuilder layer

`BgfxFrameBuilder` vtable **RVA `0xf2bdeb0`** [V] (the symbol run before it, `0xf2bde70`, belongs to another class: slot 0 is `0x67ec9e0`). 114 slots; LeviLamina's 110-slot list is older, +1 shift appears around `startFrame`, +4 by `_insert` [V by strings].

| Slot (offset) | Function | Note |
|---|---|---|
| 17 (+0x88) | `0x661f200` | likely `startFrame`/`loadAsyncAssets` ("RenderDragon Missing Textures") [I] |
| 18 (+0x90) | `0x66232b0` size 0xB3D0 | **`BgfxFrameBuilder::endFrame`** [V] ("Persistent UI Color", "Deferred Texture Transactions"); lambda `0x67c9c20` |
| 107 (+0x358) | `0x6674050` | `_insert(Editor)` |
| 108 (+0x360) | `0x666aa00` | `_insert(Effect)` |
| 109 (+0x368) | `0x6664760` | `_insert(UI)` |
| **110 (+0x370)** | `0x6655de0` | `_insert(Sky)` |
| **111 (+0x378)** | `0x66466e0` | `_insert(Item)` = terrain, actors, skinned meshes, nametags, cracks, item-in-hand |
| 112 (+0x380) | `0x6640ae0` | `_insert(Light)` |
| 113 (+0x388) | `0x768f0` | `_initiateTDR` (shared stub) |

`insert<T>` clones are 0x137-byte stamped functions: they put `{desc*, variantIndex}` on the stack and call the group's `_insert` slot through the CFG thunk `[0xF99FFA0]`. The variant index is the index inside the group, so Item idx 3 (terrain) and Sky idx 3 (end sky) collide; the group is the slot offset. (Existing `FrameFilterHook` keys on call sites for this reason.)

Group variants (from LeviLamina, index order confirmed for Item by the 11-entry jump table):
- **Sky (0x370)**: Atmosphere, Clouds, Cubemap, EndSky, LegacyCubemap, Sky, Stars, CelestialBody, VolumetricFog, Weather, SkyAmbientSamples
- **Item (0x378)**: 0 EnvironmentalText, 1 Nametag, 2 MeshFallback, 3 ChunkGeometry, 4 ChunkOccluders, 5 Crack, 6 ItemInHand, **7 SkinnedMesh**, 8 BlockSelectionOverlayBlockEntity, 9 BlockChanges, **10 BatchActor**
- Light (0x380): LightAmbient/Directional/Point, GameplayMetadata, ShadowAtlasFrame, ServerGraphicsOverride, VoxelVolume
- UI (0x368): Gameface, HudIcon, UIMesh, UIText, UIImGui
- Effect (0x360): FlameBillboard, BlitFlipbook, Particle, PlayerVision, Shadow, FadeToBlack, AimAssistHighlight, FullscreenEffect, EditorHighlight
- Editor (0x358): block-volume boxes, gizmo handles, selection cursor, render plane

`BgfxFrameBuilder` fields seen in `_insert(Item)`: `+0x20` registry/frame owner, `+0x28` BgfxContext (slot 0x370 getter forwards `[this+0x28]` into `0xc270c10`), `+0xF0`, `+0x100`, `+0x260`, `+0x268`, `+0x358`, `+0x36C`, `+0x370`, `+0x380`.

## 5. Actors in bgfx

Game side to description (1.26.62.01):
- `ActorRenderDispatcher::render(BaseActorRenderContext&, Actor&, ...)` (LeviLamina layout in `ActorRenderDispatcher.h`; `mDataDrivenRenderers`/`mNonDataDrivenRenderers`) -> `DataDrivenRenderer` -> `insert<RenderBatchActorDescription>`.
- `insert<RenderBatchActorDescription>` clone `0x4e39cb0` (variant idx 0xA); callers `0x6ebb510` (size 0x1010A, contains `variable.gliding_speed_value` = the data-driven actor renderer body) and `0x4e355a0`.
- `insert<RenderSkinnedMeshDescription>` clone `0x4e39f20` (profiler "FrameBuilder - Insert"); caller `0x575efc0` (player renderer: `minecraft:player.{}.{}`, `#custom_rot_y`); inline in `0x6b84e60` (callers `0x6b80870`, `0x6b80f00`, `0x6e8dff0` "Untagged Tessellator Mesh").
- `insert<RenderNametagDescription>` clone `0x1127330` (caller `0x11276b0`); terrain clone `0x15a6880`; sky clone `0x5369b30`.

Extraction (frame builder side):
- `_insert(Item)` `0x66466e0` builds a context from the builder fields and calls the **visitor `0x6646980`**: variant idx -> jump table `0xf2bf2d0`: 0 `0x6646a10`, 1 `0x6646f5b`, 2 `0x6646c8d`, 3 `0x6646d5f`, 4 `0x6646a89`, 5 `0x66471de`, 6 `0x6647320`, 7 `0x6646dc2` (skinned mesh, inline; branches on material-name hash `0xD8EF928BC853553A`, `0x2C8B246FD3EDDC59`, `0x416AE22C...`), 8 `0x66473e6` -> `0x66e75f0` (BlockSelectionOverlayBlockEntity), 9 `0x6646bc6`, **10 `0x6647383` -> `0x66d9af0` = BatchActor extractor** (size 0x8BB7).
- `0x66d9af0` takes the description (`+0xE0/+0xE8` = array of instances, stride 0x48, each holds an actor/entity id at `+0x40`), keeps a sorted id list (binary search + insert), then binds per-draw material parameters by name: `TintedAlphaTestEnabled`, `MetalnessUniform`, `EmissiveUniform`, `RoughnessUniform`, `SubsurfaceUniform`, `PBRTextureFlags`, `ActorFPEpsilon`, `DitheringEnabledToggle`, `MultiplicativeTintColor`, `UVScale`, `GlintColor`, `UseAlphaRewrite`, `PrevBones`, `PrevWorld`, textures `MERSTexture`, `NormalTexture`, `MatTexture1/2`. So an actor draw = batched instances + bone palette (`PrevBones` for motion vectors) + MERS PBR textures + a tint/glint block; material variants are chosen by material hash index (see memory: pass identity, 5136 DXBC blobs).
- Skinned path additionally has BVH/ray-tracing forms: `PreBlasBGFXSkinning` (`0xf5b4930` string), `bgfxbridge::BVHSkinnedMesh`, `BVHMesh`, `_preBlasSkinDispatch`.
- Output: render objects tagged `SceneObjectActors` (+ `NonShadowOnlyObject`, shadow caster tags) in the entt registry, drawn by `extractAndDrawWithExclusion<Opaque|AlphaTest|Transparent, SceneObjectActors>` lambdas (`0x126ca320`, `0x126cdb00` RTTI) and into shadow cascades via `DynamicShadowCaster`+`ActorShadow`.
- Entity creation parameters: `bgfxbridge::EntityCreationContext`.

## 6. Where to hook (optimization module seams)

1. `FrameBuilder::insert<T>` clones / group `_insert` slots: cull, LOD, drop whole classes before extraction (actors: `0x4e39cb0`, `0x4e39f20`; Item group slot +0x378).
2. `0x66d9af0` BatchActor extractor: per-instance filter (distance, frustum, id) with no bgfx involvement.
3. `0xc270460` encode frame graph / `0x66232b0` endFrame: pass-level enable/disable, resolution scale (see `bluey-perf-upscaling-re`).
4. `RendererContextD3D12::submit` `0xe82e230`: per-draw statistics, state dedup, instancing merge (highest risk, 0x4D91 bytes).
5. bgfx core `0xbc735b0` / `0xbc66de0`: texture creation (mip bias, format downgrade).

## 6b. Live findings (Cheat Engine, game in main menu, base 0x7FF689120000)

- **Correction:** global `0x12BB4910` does not hold a `bgfx::Context`; it holds the **`RendererContextD3D12` object** itself (`this` of submit; first qword = vtable at RVA `0xF4EEE80`, the "Direct3D 12 RTX"-named class, even on an AMD 760M iGPU). `+0xCC9268` = wrapper whose first qword is a d3d12.dll vtable (the command queue). `bgfx::Context` (the >0x1EBF2F1-byte object in `0xbc63690`) is a separate object, global not yet located. Section 1's "Context" rows labelled `Context +0x400` etc. are `RendererContextD3D12` offsets.
- `submit` (`0xe82e230`), `BgfxFrameBuilder::endFrame` (`0x66232b0`) and `dragon::platform::BgfxContext::endFrame` (`0xc270460`) fire **1:1:1 per frame** [V].
- `submit(rcx=RendererContext, rdx=Frame*, r8, r9)`: `rdx` alternates between **two Frame buffers 0xE749C0 bytes apart** (double-buffered: render/submit); r8/r9 are two small helper objects (clear-quad / text blitter style).
- **Frame** starts with the identity `u16 viewRemap[...]` table (0,1,2,...127...) like upstream bgfx; the per-view block at `+0xDC57E0` holds a count (0x56), pointers, and the 1920x1137 viewport (backbuffer) [V].
- `endFrame` rcx = BgfxFrameBuilder object, `[rcx+0x28]` = the BgfxContext passed on to `0xc270460` (`rax = rcx+0x28`) [V].

## 6c. Live actor findings (singleplayer world, Vibrant Visuals on)

- `insert<RenderBatchActorDescription>` (`0x4e39cb0`) fires **once per frame** (317 hits vs 313 `submit`s), and so does the extractor `0x66d9af0` (314): the whole frame's actors go in ONE batch description, not one insert per actor. `insert<RenderSkinnedMeshDescription>` clone `0x4e39f20` did not fire in this scene (no player model in view; first person).
- `insert` clone `rcx` = BgfxFrameBuilder object (same as endFrame's `rcx`), `rdx` = description pointer; **the description lives on the caller's stack** (`0x149FD0`-style low stack addresses, CE shows them truncated to 32 bits), so it cannot be read after the call returns. The extractor receives it as `r8` and the variant `{desc*, idx=0xA}` as `rdx` context. A register/stack snapshot suggests the instance count at desc `+0xE0` was small (7 or 22 in two readings; alignment of the snapshot was by hand, **unverified**) and the array at `+0xE8` was not an array of 0x48-byte structs when read (it looked like 16-byte {ptr, ptr} pairs): treat the `+0xE0/+0xE8` layout as **unconfirmed**; the code does `lea rcx,[rbx+rbx*8]` (stride 0x48) on `[desc+0xE8]`, so that is the field to re-probe.
- **Method (works):** CE `auto_assemble` code cave at the insert clone entry (`Minecraft.Windows.exe+4E39CB0`, 11 original bytes `55 56 57 53 48 81 EC C8 00 00 00`) that `rep movsb`-copies `[rdx]` (0x300 bytes) and the instance array into an `alloc`'d buffer, then restores. CE logging breakpoints alone are too late for stack-resident descriptions. No DLL probe needed.
- **`RenderBatchActorDescription` layout (CONFIRMED live):** `+0xE0` u64 = instance count (7 in this scene), `+0xE8` = pointer to the instance array, element stride **0x48**. Description head `+0x00..` is a float block (7 floats: 0.67, 0.82, 0.99999, 1.0, 0.60, 1.0, 192.0 = parameters/uniform defaults) followed by identity-like 1.0 floats; `+0x70`.. flags and pointers.
- **Instance element (0x48 bytes):** `+0x00` ptr to per-instance render object (objects of 0x300 bytes, contiguous in one allocation); `+0x08` ptr to a shared **geometry/model resource** (the extractor reads its key at `+0x40`; elements with the same model share it: 4 elements -> one resource, 3 -> another); `+0x10` ptr (resource-side, 0x10 below the geometry resource); `+0x18` u16 and flags (8/6/2), then 6 floats (bounds: -5, -0, 0, 5, 16, 1) and packed half/short values (bone/uv transform). The geometry resource has a vtable at RVA-region `0x7FF69836CB00` (rdata), refcounts at `+0x08/+0x0C`, and mesh pointers at `+0x10/+0x18`.
- Hit rate: 40 captures in ~1 s of idle => the insert runs per frame, count of 7 actors in view.

### 6d. Batch instance contents (live, 2026-11-11, count 1 in first person)

- Instance (0x48): `+0x00` ptr to a 0x300 render record, `+0x08/+0x10` model resource (+0x10 is the resource's 0x10-lower twin), `+0x18` u32 flags (0xef000006), `+0x1C..+0x34` local-space AABB floats (min -5.75,-4.6,-11.8 / max 5.75,12,7.75 for the sampled model), `+0x38` two 0.000488 (1/2048) scale floats, `+0x40` packed halves (uv/bone transform).
- Render record (0x300): mostly handles and material bits: `+0x38` int 256, `+0x40/+0x48` ptr pair, `+0x178..+0x198` FLT_MAX/-FLT_MAX bounds-reset sentinels, `+0x1D0..+0x258` index/slot table full of 0xFFFF (unbound texture/uniform slots), `+0x268..+0x278` ptr triple, `+0x298..+0x2B8` copy of the instance AABB/scale block, `+0x2F0` RVA 0xf2e4ed0 (a vtable), `+0x2F8` = {1,1}.
- **There is no actor pointer, entity id or world position in either structure**: a batch instance is a model/draw record, not an actor. World transforms travel elsewhere (per-draw uniforms / the registry), so per-actor culling at this insert needs a different identity route (the existing pass-identity item-key table in RenderMarkHook, or the extractor's registry components).

## 6e. Frame layout (live dump of one Frame, 1.26.62.01) [V = confirmed in dump, I = inferred]

Frame pointer = `rdx` of submit (0xe82e230); two buffers alternate (0xE749C0 apart, the next Frame's identity table starts at +0xE749C0). `rcx` of submit = the context object (`RendererContextD3D12`, vtable 0xF4EEE80 = the "RTX"-named subclass).
- `+0x000` u16 `viewRemap[256]` = identity (0,1,2,...) [V]; `+0x200..` array of per-view 4x4 float matrices (identity-like at idle), stride 0x40 [I].
- `+0xB4000..+0xCA000` array of **0x80-byte draw records** [V stride by autocorrelation; 682 non-empty records in the sample, 527 draws counted]: `+0x00` u32 (uniform/constant offset), `+0x04` u16 pair, `+0x20` u32 0x27 (state), `+0x24` u32 0x50000001, `+0x34` u32 0x50, `+0x38` u32 0x164 (program?), `+0x3C` u32 counting up per draw (0x170, 0x171, ... = draw/bind index), `+0x40` u32 and `+0x44` u32 varying (start index / index count) [I], `+0x48` u32 2, `+0x4C` u32 varying (vertex count?) [I], `+0x5C` u16 pair 0xFFFF,1, `+0x74..+0x78` u16 6, 0xFFFF [I]. This is bgfx's RenderDraw in Mojang's layout; the existing RenderMarkHook knows the encoder-side copy (header at encoder+0x40, 0x80 bytes, marker byte +0x1F).
- `+0x934178 + n*0x78` region is indexed through the CONTEXT, not the Frame (see context fields below). `+0x934000..+0x951000` holds 0x78-stride per-view/per-pass records [I].
- Tail block (view/frame params): `+0xDC57E0` ptr, `+0xDC57E8` u32 count (0x20F in the sample) [I], `+0xDC57EC` u16 limit, `+0xDC57F0` u32 transient index buffer size, `+0xDC57F4` u32 transient vertex buffer size (96), `+0xDC57F8` ptr TransientIndexBuffer (u16 handle at +0x10), `+0xDC5800` ptr TransientVertexBuffer (u16 handle at +0x12), `+0xDC5808` {u32 width, u32 height} = 1920x1137 [V], `+0xDC5810` u32 reset flags 0x400080 [I].
- `+0xE60DE0..+0xE60EC0` queue/cursor block written in submit's end (`+0xE60DF0..+0xE60E30` pointers, `+0xE60E48..+0xE60E58` dwords, `+0xE60EC0` u16 count) [I].
- `+0xE746C8/+0xE746D0` u64 pair (counter + capacity), `+0xE746D8` u32 **frame counter** (23285 in the sample, rises by 1 per frame) [V by read in submit r9d], `+0xE746E8..+0xE74910` ~0x230 bytes of float parameters (fog, exposure, tonemap-like values 0.1, 0.02, 0.8, 0.75, 8.0, 40.0) [I].
Context (`rcx`) fields used by submit: `+0x19F88` int renderer state (<5 = skip) [I], `+0x2D8` ptr, `+0x410` sub-object, `+0x19F58` ptr to the **resource tables** (guarded paged heap arrays: allocation bitmaps at `+0x48`/`+0xA258`, per-handle state dwords at `+0x24C`/`+0xA45C`, handle->resource arrays at `+0xA248` stride 0x80, `+0x30`/`+0x38` backing stores) [I], `+0x238` ptr, `+0x93A178` array of 0x78-byte per-handle records [I].

### 6e-2. Draw header fields (D3D12 draw executor 0xe821120, size 0xC3C3; the record builder returns at 0xE82CB90) [V = read in the code, I = inferred]

The executor turns each 0x80-byte header (`[rbx+0xf0]` in its frame) into a D3D12 draw. Fields it reads: `+0x40` u32 start index [I]; `+0x44` u32 index count, `0xFFFFFFFF` = computed as buffer size / index stride [V]; `+0x48` u32 index stride 2 or 4, selects DXGI_FORMAT_R16_UINT (0x39) or R32_UINT (0x2A) [V]; `+0x4C` u32 vertex count [I]; `+0x50` u32 byte offset added to the vertex buffer base, `+0x54` u32 count, `+0x58` u16 stride -> the D3D12_VERTEX_BUFFER_VIEW {BufferLocation, SizeInBytes = +0x58*+0x54, StrideInBytes} (the order of +0x54/+0x58 follows the executor's `imul`) [V]; `+0x5A` u16 index buffer handle (`<<5` indexes the 0x20-stride index buffer table at context `+0x93A178`) [V]; `+0x76` u16 vertex buffer handle (0xFFFF = none; `<<7` indexes a 0x80-stride vertex buffer record array, base global rva near `[rip+0x4387d56]`) [V]. The record the executor builds (0x88 bytes in RenderMarkHook's notes) copies `+0x40` to record `+0x78`, index count to `+0x70`, `+0x54` to `+0x74`. Draw call is issued through the command-list wrapper (`[wrapper+0x30]` object, vtable `+0x158` draw, `+0x1d8` set-vertex-buffers, `+0xd0` resource barrier). SDK: `src/sdk/mc/deps/bgfx/Frame.h`, `RendererContext.h`.

## 6f. RendererContext vtables (65 slots, RendererContextI)

- Base D3D12 context vtable **`0xF4ED2D0`** [V]; the live object uses the "Direct3D 12 RTX" subclass vtable **`0xF4EEE80`**; submit is slot 50 of the base (`0xe82e230`), slot 50 of the RTX class is a 0x381-byte override (`0xe83d440`) that wraps it. `tools/sigs/vtdump.py`-style listings are in this section's data below.
- Slots the RTX class overrides: 0 dtor (0xe84b280), 1, 4 getRendererName ("Direct3D 12 RTX", 0xe83d200), 10, 14, 15, 16, 19, 21, 26, 50 submit, 54 (new, 0xe83d7d0 size 0x671), 55 (0xe83df70), 58 init (0xe83e200), 59 (0xe83e980), 60 (0xe83ec70, "DLSSOptimalSettingsCallback"), 63 (0xe83f1d0 size 0x107b, "Type Mismatch in Vertex format used for GPU skinning Vertices of Actor": GPU-skinned vertex update / BLAS build), 64 (0xe8403d0 size 0xAA5B, "SuperSampling.*": DLSS/upscaler init and evaluate). Slots 63/64 are RTX-only (base points at the shared stub 0x768f0).
- Base slots with real bodies (size, guesses by bgfx's RendererContextI order, [I]): 2/3 type/caps getters, 4 getRendererName (0xe80f610), 5 isDeviceRemoved, 6 (0xe811fb0, 0x504), 7 (0xe8102b0, 0x1924, 110 calls: strings "Invalid Shader", "Failed to create resource", "Unable Initialize": large create/initialize helper), 22 createShader (0xe8137b0: "MemoryReader: read truncated.", "Unknown shader format %x"), 23/24 destroy/createProgram-like, 26/28/30-35 resource create/update with the guarded-paged-array handle checks, 36-37, 39-45 (texture/framebuffer/uniform family), 50 submit, 51 (0xe818bc0, 0xB87) readback/blit-like, 52, 55-57, 58 init (0xe807ff0, d3d12.dll / CreateEventExA), 59 (0xe80dec0, 0xDAD, 86 calls: shutdown/reset), 60-62. Exact names need bgfx-order matching per slot (open item).

## 6i. Live per-frame insert rates (server with many players, Vibrant Visuals OFF / Fancy, 1.26.62.01, measured with CE code caves on the thin insert clones)

Counters over 265 frames (LightDirectional and BlitFlipbook fire once per frame, so they count frames): Nametag **~35 inserts/frame** (clone 0x1127330), UIMesh ~25/frame (0x4c96140), ChunkGeometry ~10/frame (0x15a6880), ItemInHand ~5.5/frame (0x531b180), Particle ~4/frame (0x5b8d520), Crack 1/frame (0x539fff0), CelestialBody 1/frame (0x5370b20), LightDirectional 1/frame (0x5370960), BlitFlipbookTexture 1/frame (0x5acbe20). Did NOT fire in this scene: Weather (0x5ad5800), SkinnedMesh clone 0x4e39f20 (players go through BatchActor or the inlined skinned insert in 0x6b84e60), ChunkOccluders, Cubemap, EndSky, CameraAimAssistHighlight, Clouds clone 0x53e88a0 (cloud and sky are inserted from the fat 0x5369b30 / other callers). Nametag insert is not (rcx=desc, rdx=desc): for the Nametag clone `[rdx]` is the frame builder's vtable (0xf2bdeb0), so its arguments are (description, builder) [V]; the other thin clones take (builder, description).
Implication for the optimization module: nametags are inserted ~35 times per frame (one per visible player/entity) and UI meshes ~25 times per frame; both are cheap to veto at the insert (FrameFilterHook already drops by kind).
Method note: the CE code cave must be 5 bytes (`jmp`) for fat clones (prologue `55 41 57 41 56` / `55 41 56 56 57`) and 11 bytes (jmp + nop 6) for thin ones (`55 56 57 53 48 81 EC C8 00 00 00`); Lua `writeBytes` restores them and `readBytes` verifies.

## 6j. bgfx::Caps (decoded live, 1.26.62.01)

Dump function: `0x660ecd0` (size 0x1008) is the bgfx Caps serializer (strings `homogenousDepth`, `maxDrawCalls`, ...). It was previously labelled "occlusion" in this map; that was wrong. Weather is `0x6745440`.

Globals (rva): supported flags u64 `0x12adfa50`; homogeneousDepth u8 `0x12adfc88`; originBottomLeft u8 `0x12adfc89`; numGPUs u8 `0x12adfc8a`; fpEpsilon float `0x12adfc94`; limits u32[27] `0x12ae0d58`.

Live values (AMD 760M iGPU, D3D12 RTX path): numGPUs 2, maxDrawCalls 65535 (matches the draw header array [0xB4000, 0x8B4000) in the Frame), maxBlits 1024, maxTextureSize 16384, layers 2048, views 256, framebuffers 128, fbAttachments 8, programs 2048, shaders 4096, textures 65535, samplers 65, vertexDecls 64, streams 4, indexBuffers/vertexBuffers 4096, uniforms 512, occlusionQueries 256, encoders 10, transient VB 6 MiB, IB 2 MiB.

Flags on: AlphaToCoverage, BlendIndependent, Compute, DrawIndirect, FragmentDepth, FragmentOrdering, Instancing, OcclusionQuery, SwapChain, Texture2DArray, Texture3D, TextureBlit, TextureCompareLequal, TextureCubeArray, TextureReadBack, VertexAttribHalf/Uint10/Sint8/Uint16, VertexId, StructuredBuffers, RayTracing, NoFragmentShader, BufferBindOffset, RangedResource, DynamicTextureHandles, GpuTimer. Off: ConservativeRaster, GraphicsDebugger, HiDPI, Index32, RendererMultithreaded, TextureDirectAccess, HDR10, DepthBias, Upscaling (bit positions in `src/sdk/mc/deps/bgfx/Caps.h`; bits 32+ are Mojang additions).

## 6l. Nametag clone 0x1127330 decoded statically

Signature (builder-last variant): (ctx rcx, builder rdx, desc r8, shared r9). Flow:
1. `0x4def080(desc)` returns bool; clone exits if false. `[r9]` (shared handle) must be non-null and `[[r9]+0x40]` non-zero.
2. `ctx+0x18` is a ring of per-frame camera/transform records: `[ring+0x78]=1` marks the record used, reads the record at `(head+count-1)&(cap-1)` where ring fields are +0x48 data*, +0x50 capacity, +0x58 head, +0x60 count, +0x68 offset, +0x70 flag. Record (0x40 bytes) is copied as 4 x 16 bytes + two qwords to the stack; when `[ring+0x70]==1` a second record (+0x30 vec2, +0x38 float) is read as the camera position; `ctx+0x74` byte is a flag, `ctx+0x20/+0x30/+0x60` are passed through.
3. `0x668b6e0(out, builder, copiedRecord, shared, ctx30, &camPos, ctx20, &flags, ctx+0x60)` builds the real description object (in-place on the stack).
4. A trace/telemetry static guard (TLS index check, skipped normally) then the variant `{ptr=desc, tag=1}` is dispatched through `builder->vtable[0x378]` (Item group). So Nametag is Item group tag 1.
Other fat clones (UIText/EnvironmentalText 0xe71330, Crack 0x539fff0, Sky/Stars 0x5369b30, Clouds 0x53e88a0, Shadow 0x53e6c30, PlayerVision 0x53e62b0) follow the same shape: validate, pull per-frame records from ring buffers in the frame context, build, dispatch.

## 6m. Nametag description object and the Item visitor table

`0x668b6e0` (called from the Nametag clone) constructs the Nametag description in place, size 0x98: +0x00 builder/owner ptr, +0x08 mat4 (16 floats, copy of the per-frame record), +0x48 vec3 (camera/position), +0x58 shared_ptr (ptr,ctrl; ctrl refcount at +8, incremented), +0x68 16 bytes copied from ctx+0x30, +0x78 qword from ctx+0x20, +0x80 u16 flags, +0x84 vec4 optional (valid flag byte at +0x94, set only when the source optional's flag byte at +0x10 is 1). `0x668b850` is a sibling constructor for a larger description (>= 0x130 bytes: mat4 at +0x90, vec3 at +0xD0, five bytes of flags at +0xDC..+0xE0, two 16-byte copies at +0xE4/+0xF4, shared_ptr at +0x108/+0x110, camera ptr +0x118, floats from `[[cam+0x100]+0x40]` at +0x120..).

Item visitor `0x6646980` signature: (this rcx, frameCtx rdx, variant r8 = {desc*, tag byte at +8}); tag > 10 jumps to the error path; frameCtx +0x40/+0x68/+0x70 are read (+0x70 == 1 gates dither/emissive style paths). Jump table 0xf2bf2d0 (relative i32 from the table): tag 0 0x6646a10, 1 0x6646f5b, 2 0x6646c8d, 3 0x6646d5f, 4 0x6646a89, 5 0x66471de, 6 0x6647320, 7 0x6646dc2, 8 0x66473e6, 9 0x6646bc6, 10 0x6647383. Strings reached from the blocks (blocks overlap in a linear scan so assignments are [I]): tag 0 and 4 reference "MeshFallbackPosUVNormalColor"/"ChunkOccluder" (mesh-fallback and chunk-occluder materials), tags 1/5 "Cracks", tags 2/3/7/9 "CracksBlockEntity" (block-entity crack/overlay draws), tag 8 "GlowInkSignForwardPBR" (sign glow text). Tags 6, 8, 10 only call `0x4df6e80` (small forwarders; 10 = BatchActor goes through it to the 0x66d9af0 extractor).

## 6n. dragon::platform::BgfxContext::endFrame (0xc270460, 0x2aa bytes) flow [V by disassembly]

Thin wrapper; real work is in callees. Sequence: (1) trace scope "BgfxContext - Generate and encode frame graph" via TLS-index guard; (2) packs `this`-fields (rbx+0x38, +0x40/+0x48 swapped 64-bit pair, +0x50) into a small struct and calls `0xc273420` (size 0x8A73: the frame-graph generate/encode function, 4 calls into 0xa78940, 8 to the allocator free 0xe935700); (3) calls `0xe9002e0(taskList, {..,+0x118 of rbx+0x20, rbx+0x58, rbx+0x18,..})` to dispatch/run the encoded tasks, then `0xeec58f0` (fence/wait helper); (4) walks the doubly linked list at `[[rbx+0x58]+0x40]` of per-view render objects: skips the one equal to `[global+0x13d18]` (the backbuffer/default view), for each: reads the Frame tail pointer table `[[view]+0xDC57E0][view+0x6ac]` (the per-view block that the Frame layout section calls +0xDC57E0; `view+0x6ac` byte = view slot), writes a u32 `1` through `0xebce390` into the block when room, zeroes `[block+4]`, stores a timestamp from `0xebdb740` into `view+0x6f8` and signals `0xebdbc50(global+0x100, 1)`; (5) frees the list head (`0xc271290` destroys the 0xa8-byte list node object, `0xe935700` frees), then `0xc283720` (tears down the temp struct, tail-calls `0xc27ed70`).
Take-away: `0xc273420` is the single function that turns the declared dragon graph into encoded tasks; `0xe9002e0` is the task dispatch; the per-view objects have `+0x6ac` view slot byte and `+0x6f8` last-present time stamp.

### 6n-2. 0xc273420 (frame graph generate/encode, 0x8A73 bytes)
No usable strings (only unordered_map length errors). 51 distinct callees. Hot ones: 0x9bc830 x42 (small-vector/arena push), 0xe935700 x27 (free), 0xa78940 x14, 0xeec5850 x9, 0xeec73f0/0xeec6* (task/handle accessors), 0xe905ce0/0xe905d00/0xe905ef0 (graph resource table ops), 0xc27f940 x3, 0xc27fcc0 x2, 0xc27ec60 x2 (sub-steps), 0x6759ea0 x2, 0xbc76610 and 0xbc752f0 (bgfx core calls, view/resource setup), 0x67bf2c0, 0xc280010 (final step). Pass identity is by hashes, so naming needs a runtime capture of the declared passes (planned: breakpoint on the sub-step 0xc27f940 and walk its argument).

## 6o. Pass names and the name hash [V]

HashedString hash = FNV-1a 64 (offset 0xcbf29ce484222325, prime 0x100000001b3), computed at runtime from the string: the VV giant function 0x55f1450 alone has 132 offset-basis loads and 240 prime multiplies. Earlier searches found no names because the pass names are NOT stored as .rdata strings: they are built as small strings by `mov reg/mem, imm` of 8-byte ASCII pieces (SSO string written inline) next to the hash code. Recover them by scanning code for printable `movabs`/`mov mem, imm` runs (tool: `scratchpad/inlnames.py <lo> <hi>`: groups immediates within 0x30 bytes and merges overlapping pieces).

Names recovered from 0x55f1450..0x563f594 (VV frame renderer pass declarations; address of first piece): "Shadow Cascades" 0x55f3871, "PopulateVolume" 0x55fdebd, "Prepass" (x8) 0x55ff932, "GeometryPrepass" (x4) 0x55ff987, "DepthOnly..." 0x5609448 (DepthOnlyOpaque 0x56272cf), "DepthBufferCopy" 0x560be42, "Blit" (x6+), "Copy" 0x560b84c, "AlphaTestCracks" 0x561050a, "Transparent" (x3), "Opaque", "ForwardPBR" (x15, 0x5612014..0x56256de..), "MotionOnly" 0x562321e, "IBLSyncPoint" 0x562be6c, "NothingTask" (x2) 0x562beba, "...Objects" 0x562e925, "DebugBlitPhase" (x4) 0x5633a06, "NameTag" 0x5634152, "Draw" 0x563e332, plus rdata-string labels listed in RENDER_CATALOG (27 VV pass labels). Fragments that did not merge ("dShadows" 0x55fc492 = ...Shadows, "ngOutput" 0x562d072 = ...Output, "dObjects"/"yObjects" 0x562e925/0x562ef41 = ...Objects, "dUI]" 0x56331cd = ...UI) need their other half from rdata or from a second string source.

Static data tables found by the same scan in 0x5700000+: D3D format name table (0x56e3a12..0x56e42f6: R32G32_FLOAT, R8G8B8A8_UNORM, D32_FLOAT, R24G8_TYPELESS, BC3/BC7, ...), blend factor and compare-func name tables (0x56e1c85, 0x56e2654), vertex attribute names (Position, Tangent, BoneId0, PBRTextureIdx, GeoType) 0x56e3068.., topology names (0x56e46f7). These are the material/pipeline-state enum string tables (G layer).

## 6p. Pass hash = FNV-1 64, pass and material name tables [V]

Correction of 6o: the HashedString hash is **FNV-1 64** (h = (h * 0x100000001b3) ^ byte, offset 0xcbf29ce484222325), not FNV-1a. Verified: fnv1("GeometryPrepass") = 0x680e1340911894f3 = the immediate stored next to the inline string at 0x55ff9a9 (HashedString = {u64 hash +0, std::string +8}). The pass declarations build the name inline and store the constexpr-folded hash as a 64-bit immediate, so a pass can be identified without running the game by matching 64-bit immediates against FNV-1 of every .rdata string plus names merged from inline immediates.

Tools/data: `docs/data/passhash2.py <lo> <hi>` (resolver), `docs/data/inlnames.py`, `docs/data/render_hashes.txt` (752 resolved hashes with first-use rva), SDK `src/sdk/mc/deps/minecraft_renderer/dragon/PassNames.h` (constexpr fnv1 + ~110 pass names + material names).

Pass names found (VV/Fancy/RTX): shadows SampleCascadedShadows, Populate(Volume), UploadDirtyVolumeKeys, UploadPerGroupLerps, CombineVoxelLighting; geometry GeometryPrepass(AlphaTest), DepthOnlyOpaque/AlphaTest, DepthAndNormal, RasterizedOpaque/AlphaTest/Transparent, ForceForwardPBROpaque, ForwardPBRAlphaTest/Transparent(+SkyProbe), AlphaTestCracks, MotionOnly, FullScreenOverlayTransparent, TransparentDegamma; lighting DoDeferredLighting, DirectionalLightingPass, DiscreteIndirectCombinedLightingPass, SurfaceRadianceUpscalePass, DoDeferredShading, DoSkyProbeDeferredShading, LightClustering, ClusterLights, DrawLights, DoIndirectSpecularShading/Upscale, VolumeScattering, AccumulateInscatter, AccumulateGIInscatter, BlurGIInscatter; water DoWaterSurfaceBuffer, WaterSurface, WaterExtinction, WaterDepthBufferBlit, CausticsMultiplierPass; SSR SSRRayMarch(HZB), SSRFillGaps, SSRGetReflectedColor, SSRColorBufferBlit; post TAAUpscaling, TAAU, FSR1Compute/Frag, BloomBlend, ThresholdedDownSample, DFDownSample/UpSample, LuminanceHistogram, BuildHistogram, CalculateAverage, ToneMappingHistogram, ApplyLocalExposure, BuildBilateralGrid/Filter/Clear, ColorPostProcess, CopyToFinal, Blit, BlitUncompressed, DebugBlitPhase, HighlightTextureEdge, CubemapToOffscreen, Convolve, IBLSyncPoint, NothingTask, GameFaceRender; RTX PreBlasSkinning(Dispatch), PrepareTLAS, PrimaryCheckerboardRayGenInline, SunShadowRayGenInline, RefractionRayGenInline, DiffuseRayGenCombinedInline, SpecularRayGenInline, ExplicitLightSamplingInline, PathTracingRayGenInline, Update(Vertex|Face)IrradianceCacheInline, CalculateInscatter/GIInscatterInline, IncidentLightMeterInline, AdaptiveDenoiser*Inline, TemporalDenoising, ShadowDenoising, FinalizeDenoising, AtrousSH, FilterMoments, BlendCheckerboardFields(+SH/Shadow), Diffuse/SpecularFireflyFilter, CheckerboardUpscale/Interleave, TileClassification, BlurGradients, FinalCombine, ReprojectSpecularOnly.
Material names (RenderMaterialGroup keys) found as hashes: item_in_hand(+_multicolor_tint,_glint,_entity_alphatest_color...), entity_alphatest, entity_alphablend(_nocolor), entity_glint, entity_static, entity.skinning, name_tag(+_with_backface, _depth_tested...), selection_overlay(_opaque/_double_sided/_block_entity.skinning), cracks_overlay(_alpha_test,_tile_entity.skinning), opaque_block(_color), terrain_inside_block, shadow_back/front/overlay, skyplane, sun_moon, end_sky, cubemap, ui_* (textured, fill_gradient, crosshair, cubemap, ...), fullscreen_cube_overlay_opaque/blend, on_screen_effect, *.skinning block entities (banner, bell, boat, chalkboard, conduit_wind, enchanting_table_book, ...).

## 6q. bgfx::Context: init + backend thread start (0xbc63690, 0x4e7 bytes) [V by disassembly]

`rcx = bgfx::Context*` (object > 0x1ec0000 bytes; contains both Frames' per-view arrays inline). Function = Context::init-tail / `renderFrame` thread setup:
- clears bit 7 of `[ctx+0x1ebf19f]` (flag byte), zeroes `[ctx+0x1ebf2ef]` and `[ctx+0x1ebf1d0]`, stores a timestamp (`0xebdb740` = QPC) at `[ctx+0x1ebf1c8]`.
- Calls `0xbc65180` on the two semaphore/event objects at `[ctx+0x1cfd0c0]` and `[ctx+0x1cfd0c8]` (api and render semaphores).
- If a global flag at `rva 0x12ae0... [rip+0x6e7e074]` is 1 (single-threaded / render-in-API-thread mode) it only reads a TLS marker; otherwise it creates the thread: `0xebdb9b0(thread obj = ctx+0x200, proc = 0xbc65720 (rip+0x1ffd), arg = ctx, name = "bgfx - renderer backend thread")`.
- Writes the TLS magic `0x78666762` ("bgfx") at TLS slot +0x2d4c (render-thread identity check used by assertions).
- Initialises the per-view array: 256 entries of stride 0x140 starting at `ctx+0x1eab080` (loop end `rbx == 0x14000`): per view +0x00 u32 0, +0x0c byte 0, +0x16 u16 1, +0x0e u64 0x1000000000000 (rect/clear defaults), +0x18 u64 0, four 4x4 float matrices (all set to identity via 64-byte clear then 1.0 on the diagonal) at +0x20, +0x60, +0xa0, +0xe0, a 16-byte zero block at +0x120, +0x130 u16 = 0xffff (frame-buffer handle = invalid), +0x132 byte 0. So the View struct is 0x140 bytes: viewMat +0x20, projMat +0x60, and two more matrices (viewProj/prev) +0xa0/+0xe0; frame buffer handle at +0x130.
- Resets handle/sort tables: `memset(ctx+0x1ea69e0, 0, 0x80)`, `memset(ctx+0x1ea6a60, 0xff, 0x2000)` and `memset(ctx+0x1ea8a60, 0xff, 0x2000)` (two 0x2000-byte 0xff tables: 4096 x u16 invalid-handle maps, e.g. view remap / program-by-shader lookup).
Use: the Context pointer is the `rcx` of `submit` (0xe82e230 context fields +0x19F58 resource tables and +0x93A178 per-handle records are relative to the RendererContext, a different object).

## 6r. bgfx::Context global and core function roles

`bgfx::s_ctx` = pointer at rva **0x12ae1748** [V]: seen as `mov rcx,[rip+0x6eb5167]` at 0xbc2c5da inside pass lambdas, and live `*(base+0x12ae1748) = 0x49c80010040` equals the Context passed through endFrame. Live reads of the view array at `ctx+0x1eab080` (stride 0x140) matched 6q: per view +0x00 u32 = clear color RGBA (v1 = 0xff000000, v3 = 0xffffffff), +0x08 float 1.0 = clear depth, +0x0c/+0x0e rect/clear flags words, +0x20 mat4 viewMatrix, +0x130 u16 framebuffer handle (0xffff = backbuffer), semaphores `[ctx+0x1cfd0c0]`, `[ctx+0x1cfd0c8]` are valid heap objects.
Pass lambdas (e.g. 0xbc2c590, 0xbc2cd20, 0xbc2d550, 0xbc2dd20, 0xbc2eb50, 0xbc2f4a0, 0xbc36140, 0xbc04dc0, 0xbc0f130; each ~0x5xx bytes; strings DoDeferredLighting / CausticsMultiplierPass) do not call bgfx API functions: they write the view state directly into `s_ctx` (`shl eax,6; lea rsi,[rax+rax*4]` = view*0x140, writes at `ctx+0x1eab090+view*0x140` = rect w/h as clamped u16 pair) and fill the Frame tail table at `+0xDC57E0`. So **bgfx::setViewRect/Clear/Transform are inlined into the dragon pass code**; there is no single API function to hook for them. Hook points are the pass lambdas (found via the pass table) or the Frame at submit.
Core functions (size): `0xbc66de0` (0x19d7) = Context::init (message "Disabling dynamic texture handles is not supported."; references Frame no +0xE746D8, +0xDC5808 backbuffer size, view array); `0xbc65800` (0x37d) = render/API thread handoff (4x QPC, semaphore wait/post 0xebdbf10/0xebdbf20/0xebdbc60, calls init when first frame); `0xbc660a0` (0x9ab) = frame()/swap sequence (17 copies 0xebce390, 5 calls to 0xbc65800); `0xbc65180` (0x59c) = frame release/reset (called on both semaphore objects at start); `0xbc65e00` (0x236) small wrapper calling 0xbc76c10; `0xbc63690` thread start (6q); `0xbc65720` thread proc; `0xbc618e0` (0x535) program/shader setup using 0xbc60d10/0xbc76610/0xbc7a7c0 (A7/A8 targets).

## 6s. 0xbc66de0 is bgfx::Context::frame()/swap, not init (correction of 6r) [V by disassembly]

Confirmed by its work: it copies the API-side view state into the submit Frame (`memcpy(frame+?, ctx+0x1eaaa60, 0x200)` = view remap u16[256]; `memcpy(.., ctx+0x1eab080, 0x14000)` = the 256 view structs; `memcpy(.., ctx+0x1ebf080, 0x100)` = per-view name/flag bytes), then releases queued destroy-handles back to the allocators and bumps the frame number (+0xE746D8). The message "Disabling dynamic texture handles is not supported." sits in this function's reset path.
Handle allocators inside Context (bgfx HandleAlloc: u16 numHandles +0, u16 maxHandles +2, dense/sparse u16 arrays after): at ctx+0x1dd90c8 (dyn index/vertex?), +0x1dd90ca, +0x1dd90cc, +0x1ddd30c region (dense array of a 4096-entry allocator: word at +0x1ddd308 = numHandles, array +0x1ddd30c), +0x1de1348/0x1de134c (next allocator), +0x1de534c/0x1de5350, +0x1dfbe94/0x1dfbe98. Each allocator is followed by its resource-record array; free-queue vectors at +0x1ddd2d0, +0x1de1310 (begin/end, element 0x10 bytes, drained by 0xbc76e80/0xebdbc90). Per-resource destroy functions called while draining: 0xbc6f2a0, 0xbc6f800, 0xbc771f0, 0xbc76e80, 0xbc5f110 (semaphore-protected release). Resource record arrays are what RendererContext slots +0x19F58 index (A8b).
Frame swap object offsets: `[ctx+0x1cfd0c0]`, `[ctx+0x1cfd0c8]` = the two Frame* (submit/render); `[ctx+0x1ebf194]` Frame ptr, `[ctx+0x1ebf19c]` frame index, `[ctx+0x1ebf19f]` flags (bit7 = in-frame), `[ctx+0x1ebf1d0]` counter, byte flags cleared at end: `+0x1df7480`, `+0x1cd6fd0`, `+0x1e7dad0`, `+0x1ebf2f3`.

## 6t. submit (0xe82e230, 0x4d91 bytes) stage map by callee order [I from call order, sizes V]

The function is one linear body: prologue (stack probe 0xe9e7970), timers (0xebdb740 QPC x2, 0xebdb760), then in address order: 0xe81d660 (0x1af, frame begin/state reset), 0xe8360f0 x3 (0x3f6, descriptor/upload ring begin), 0xbc61e20 (0x215, bgfx-side view/frame prep), 0xe818490 x3 (0x4e6, per-view setup: viewport/scissor/RT bind), 0xe820910 (0x80c, clear/RT transitions), 0xe83b3d0 x2 (0x3d0), 0xe815590 (0x2af, render target resolve), 0xbc5f110 x4 (semaphore release/wait), 0xe83abb0 x3 (0x12f), 0xe82d960 x2 (0x359 PSO/state lookup candidate), 0xe81af20 / 0xe81ac80 (0x1c8/0x18c root-sig/heap binding), **0xe833210 x2 (0xde4: per-draw record build, the biggest helper)**, 0xe800c10 x21 (0x58: D3D12 command wrapper, 21 emission sites: barriers/draws/dispatches), 0xebcf580 x12 (0x141 copy/memcpy helper), 0xe834040 (0x1a3), 0xe83b0e0 (0x11c), 0xe83aed0 x4 (0x201, descriptor copy), 0xe81b3b0 (0x118), and the tail helpers for stats (0xe938cb0/0xe938d70 x8 TLS trace scopes), 0xe81d850 (0x1cd frame end/present prep). 70 indirect calls through `[rip+..]` are D3D12 COM calls (command list/queue/device methods via import-like thunks at rva ~0x1171xxx).
Draw header executor `0xe821120` (0xc3c3 bytes) is the function that reads the 0x80-byte draw headers (6e-2) and issues the D3D12 draws; it is a sibling, not part of submit's body. Detail still open: the exact loop boundaries and the PSO key bits (B3b, B4).

## 6u. BatchActor extractor 0x66d9af0 (0x8bb7 bytes) callee map

51 distinct callees. Uniform names it binds (by .rdata strings at 0x66df637..0x66dff25 and 0x66e0587): TintedAlphaTestEnabled, MetalnessUniform, EmissiveUniform, RoughnessUniform, SubsurfaceUniform, PBRTextureFlags, ActorFPEpsilon, DitheringEnabledToggle, MultiplicativeTintColor, UVScale, GlintColor, UseAlphaRewrite, PrevBones, PrevWorld, MERSTexture, NormalTexture, MatTexture1, MatTexture2.
Per-variant draw-list builders (the `extractAndDrawWithExclusion<Opaque|AlphaTest|Transparent,...>` instantiations, each 0x114f..0x13f2 bytes): `0x66cc7a0`, `0x66ce8d0`, `0x66c7680`, `0x66c98e0` (0x13f2 each), `0x66bf290`, `0x66c3840`, `0x66c1920`, `0x66c5760` (0x12e6 each), `0x66bd430` (0x11f0), `0x66bb7b0` (0x114f); called from 0x66e1371..0x66e21b3. Other helpers: 0x4df8370 (0xaf6), 0xbb68930 (0x899), 0xbcaba00 x5 (0x83f), 0x67da040 (0x6ad), 0x66cba10, 0x66a08b0, 0x66a0f60, 0x66d9710 x9 (0x343), 0x19932c0 x30 (0x32d: **XXH64(data rcx, len rdx, seed r8)**, verified by the prime constants 0xc2b2ae3d27d4eb4f/0x9e3779b185ebca87/0x85ebca77c2b2ae63/0x27d4eb2f165667c5; used 30 times: the extractor hashes keys (material/variant names) with XXH64), 0xbbd7c70, 0x66b2b30 (shared with the Nametag/Cracks handlers: entity registry "emplace" helper), 0xe86b370, 0x67743c0, 0x4e019a0.
Identity route [I]: actors are registry entities; the accessor to follow next is not 0x19932c0 (that is XXH64), look at the callees 0x66d9710 (x9) and 0xbcaba00 (x5). The per-instance mModel record has no actor id, so identity has to come from the entity components (SceneObjectActors, NameTag...) iterated here.

## 6y. Material binary facts already in the repo (docs/LCE_GRAPHICS_GAPS.md)
`.material.bin` files are containers of shader programs per backend: e.g. RenderChunk.material.bin has 480 containers (120 each of SM4, SM5, DXIL vertex, DXIL pixel; 60 unique DXIL hashes per stage); the full installed map is 157 files with 5,136 unique shader hashes; pixel/vertex programs reflect up to 23 resource bindings (ForwardPBR). The loader is 0xc26caa0 (6x) which reads `materials.index.json` then each `.material.bin`. Shader programs are keyed by 64-bit hash, XXH64 is the extractor-side hash helper (0x19932c0, 6u). Format parsing code for these containers lives in the LCE tooling under tools/ (see that doc); a runtime-verified header layout is still to be written.
Session note 2026-11-12: game process unchanged across a server reconnect (base 0x7FF7CB8E0000, bgfx ctx 0x49c80010040, no CE patches active), so live captures remain valid.
Existing tooling for that format: `tools/lce/inspect_material.py` (scans for DXBC containers by magic, classifies SM4/SM5/DXIL by chunk), `tools/lce/map_material.py`, runtime side `src/client/features/render/tracker/MaterialIndex.{h,cpp}` and `src/client/features/render/lce/LceTerrain.*`. The .material.bin is a sequence of embedded DXBC containers with a small header/index per program (the exact header is not decoded; the tools locate containers by the "DXBC" magic).

## 6z. RendererContextD3D12 resource tables (per-handle records) [V strides/bases by disassembly, roles partly I]

Tables live inside the RendererContext object (`this` of the vtable slots, NOT bgfx::Context). Access pattern (e.g. createTexture, slot 26, 0xe8143c0): record address = `this + base + handle * stride`; the first 0x1000 handles are inline in the object; handles >= 0x1000 go through the "guarded paged array": `chain = [this+0xc96178] -> [*chain]`, page size = 0x1000*stride, pages linked through a pointer stored at `page + 0x1000*stride` (loop `mov rcx,[rcx+0xb8000]` for stride 0xb8, with a magic-multiply to compute page index), a byte at `this+0xc96190` is the guard flag (non-zero reports "FAILED: accessing a guarded paged heap backed array out of bounds." through 0xbc5f110). The slot first calls `0xe83abb0(this+base, handle)` = bounds/guard check.
Tables (base, stride, users):
- **Textures**: base `this+0xbde178`, stride **0xB8**, 4096 inline entries (ends 0xC5A178); used by slots 26 createTexture (u16 handle edx, Memory* r8 = {data,size}, flags r9d), 28 updateTexture, 30 readTexture, 31 resize, 32-34 overrideInternal/getInternal/destroyTexture, 35 createFrameBuffer (attachments look up textures), 51 blit.
- **Per-handle 0x78 records**: base `this+0x93a178` (users slots 10/16/17 create/dyn buffers, 36/37, 50 submit, 52, 55-57); table ends at 0x9b2178 (0x78000 = 4096 x 0x78).
- Next tables by base spacing from slot 59 (shutdown/free-all): `0x9b2178` (0x80-byte stride, 4096 entries to 0xa32178), `0xa32178` (0x130-byte stride, 4096 entries to 0xb62178), `0xb62178` (to 0xbde178, 0x7c000 bytes = 2048 x 0xF8: the 0xF8 records used by slot 24 createProgram), `0x57a178` and `0x57a100` (0x3c0000 bytes up to 0x93a178 = 32768 x 0x78, dynamic/transient buffer records). Roles of the 0x80 and 0x130 tables are [I] (vertex layouts / uniforms or shaders / frame buffers).
- Other fields: `this+0xcc9268` command queue wrapper (all create/update slots touch it), `this+0xc97698` (0x68 stride: 0x68-byte records for slots 6, 39-41: vertex layout / uniform registry), `this+0xc9baa0` (slot 43), `this+0x19f78`/`0x19f58` init-time tables (slot 58), `this+0x14420`/`0x14458`/`0x2c6a8` (small arrays referenced by createTexture and the 0x68-record slots).
Practical use: to read texture N from a hook, `rec = renderer + 0xbde178 + N*0xB8` for N < 0x1000.
Live check (2026-11-12): renderer object `*(base+0x12BB4910)` = 0x49c84010000 with vtable rva 0xf4eee80 (the "Direct3D 12 RTX" subclass); `obj+0xbde178+h*0xB8` gives plausible texture records: +0x00 qword (ptr/id), +0x08 u32 3 (state/type), +0x18 width (0x100, 0x40, 0x80 seen), +0x20 height (u32) with +0x24 u32 flags (0x10080/0x10040/0x10010), +0x28 DXGI_FORMAT (0xb R16G16B16A16_UNORM, 0x1c R8G8B8A8_UNORM seen), +0x2c u32 1. Guard byte `obj+0xc96190` = 0. So the table layout above is confirmed on the live object.

### 6z-2. Live texture census (Fancy renderer, in a server, 1920x1137 window) [V]
Texture table scan of handles 0..0xfff: **1465 live textures**. Almost all are plain content textures (flags 0x10001, DXGI 0x1c R8G8B8A8_UNORM): 398 of 64x64, 182 of 128x128, 167 of 32x32, 163 of 16x16, 149 of 64x32 (atlases/UI sprites/mob skins). Only 18 have other flags (RT/depth/special): h1 256x256 R16G16B16A16 fl0x10080, h2 64x32 fl0x10008, h3 256x256 fmt0x3d fl0x10040 (depth/shadow-like), h8-h11 1x1 fl0x10002/6/c (dummy textures with different usage flags), h15/h21/h102 128x128 fmt0xa fl0x80006, h28 2048x2048 fmt0x35 (BC7?) fl0x10003, h29-h32 small fmt0x29/0xa fl0x10020 (20x12, 9x5: lookup/LUT), h118 32x32 fl0x10040, h125 128x128 fmt0x1a fl0x80012, h1059 1024x1024 fl0x20001. The main color target is 1920x1137 fmt 0x1a (R11G11B10_FLOAT) x2. Flag bits seen: 0x1 sampled, 0x2/0x4/0x8 usage variants, 0x20 UAV-like, 0x40 depth, 0x80 render target, 0x10000/0x20000/0x80000 mip/array/msaa variants [I]. So in Fancy mode the frame uses very few render targets; the VV passes need many more (they are not allocated now).

## 6aa. Live per-frame call order of the Fancy renderer path (CE entry-counter trace, all patches restored) [V]

Method: 39 function entries patched with a 5-byte jmp into a code cave that appends a function index to a ring buffer (`lock xadd` counter), run for a few seconds in a server (Fancy, 18 frames sampled, 6,532 hits), then ring read via Lua and all original bytes restored and verified. Function set + indices: `scratchpad/passfns2.json`; cave generator in the scratch scripts (pattern in the doc history). Also traced first: the 35 pass lambdas of the VV/RTX region 0xbb30000..0xbcd0000 (names from 6p): **none of them ran** in this scene except `NothingTask` 0xbbbfc50 (~31 hits): the Fancy frame does not use the VV/RTX pass lambdas at all.
Calls per 18 frames: item visitor 0x6646980 2413 (~134/frame), 0x66ea3d0 terrain pass 144 (8/frame), 0x678c4c0 RenderChunk 342 (19/frame), skinned-mesh clone 0x6b84e60 507 (~28/frame), flame clone 0x594cbe0 325 (18/frame, 17-in-a-row bursts), batch-actor extractor 0x66d9af0 and actor-body 0x6ebb510 / actor 0x4e355a0 (1/frame each), player renderer 0x575efc0 (1/frame), particle clone 0x5b8d520 + particle pass 0x67417f0 (4/frame), volfog clone 0x53e3aa0, player-vision clone 0x53e62b0, shadow clone 0x53e6c30 (1/frame each), crack 0x539fff0 (1/frame), mesh-fallback clone 0x4def480, sky/stars clone 0x5369b30 (1/frame, last). 
One frame, in order (game-side insert/visit order): volfog(0x53e3aa0) -> player vision(0x53e62b0) -> {particle clone, particle pass} x4 -> shadow clone(0x53e6c30) -> crack clone(0x539fff0) -> item visitor x105 (chunk geometry/occluder/block entity visits) -> arms/hands(0x532b5a0) -> flame clone -> visitor -> player renderer(0x575efc0) -> skinned clone x2 -> mesh fallback(0x4def480) -> visitor -> terrain 0x66ea3d0 / RenderChunk 0x678c4c0 interleaved -> visitor + skinned (actor models, interleaved) -> flame clone x17 -> skinned/actor body (0x4e355a0, 0x6ebb510) -> visitor x9 -> batch-actor extractor(0x66d9af0) -> sky/stars clone(0x5369b30).
Reading: the game thread submits Effect/UI/Light first, then the Item group in bursts (visitor), actors, and the sky last; the visit order is not draw order (draw order is decided by the dragon passes after endFrame).

## 6ab. The Frame renderer entry and the Fancy-path function set (live trace of 251 functions in 0x6600000-0x6800000, restored) [V]

**0x67c9c20 (size 0x966), string "Frame renderer - Frame"** is the top-level per-frame function of the renderer. It calls, in order: `0xeec58f0` (timing/wait), 7 x `0xa784c0` (setup of the 7 frame-renderer resource sets), D3D/COM calls, `0xe8feb10` PipelineDiskCache::tick (0x67ca1c1), `0xc267b90`, `0xc270330`, **`0xc270460` BgfxContext::endFrame (0x67ca3cd)**, `0x4305e0`, `0x67ca960`, `0x67cab00`, then the trace scope with the string. So endFrame (6n) is called from here, and 0xc273420 (6n-2) runs inside it.
Functions that ran in a sampled Fancy window (76 of 251), with strings: material/pass draw functions: 0x6698e30 ItemInHandForwardPBR/ItemInHandPrepass (0x5fb4), 0x66560f0 LegacyCubemapForwardPBR/CubemapHDRi/Cubemap (0x86be), 0x666aca0 Flipbook/UI/Base/CameraAimAssistHighlight (0x6c7c), 0x6664a00 Debug/ImGui, UI/Blit (0x3b10), 0x674a3a0 UI/Text, UI/Glint, UI/Blit (0x4c66), 0x66a6830 ShadowVolume/ShadowOverlay (0x2097), 0x67417f0 ParticleForwardPBR/ParticlePrepass, 0x66ea3d0 EndPortalForwardPBR/EndPortal/WaterPrepass/RenderChunk, 0x67aa700 RenderChunkFogAlpha, 0x6722cb0 (0x77b4, 4 hits), 0x6605980 BiomeBlendingTexture, 0x6753b50 uniforms MinecraftTimeOfDay/ElapsedFrameTime/CameraFacingDirection, 0x661f200 "RenderDragon Missing Textures", 0x6607b80 "UI Quad Mesh"/"UI Cursor Mesh", 0x66232b0 BgfxFrameBuilder::endFrame body ("Persistent UI Color/Depth", "Deferred Texture Transactions"), 0x67c9c20 "Frame renderer - Frame", 0x66d9af0 batch-actor extractor.
High-frequency helpers (per 4096-hit window): 0x675d430 / 0x675f3b0 / 0x675fb30 (536 each: per-draw material setup chain), 0x66a0f60 (444), 0x6646980 Item visitor (244), 0x67cfd80 (169), 0x66a08b0 "TerrainInfo" (143), 0x66a02d0 (134), 0x67dc7e0 (134), 0x6762270 (127), 0x66c98e0 (103, one of the BatchActor draw-list builders 6u), 0x66d9710 (97), 0x67d7410 (96).
First-occurrence order in the window: 0x67d14e0 0x675f3b0 0x675fb30 0x6762270 0x668cb70 0x6646980 0x6698e30 0x67cfd80 0x66a08b0 0x66a0f60 0x675d430 0x67d0750 0x67d0b00 0x66560f0 0x6640d80 0x675e440 0x67ea0d0 0x6606580 0x6609f20 0x666aca0 0x67417f0 0x67e8900 0x66a6830 0x67d50c0 0x66a02d0 0x67dc7e0 0x66042b0 0x6664a00 0x674a3a0 ... 0x66232b0 (endFrame body) ... 0x66ea3d0 ... 0x66d9af0.
Conclusion: the Fancy and VV/RTX renderers are the same framework (shared Frame renderer 0x67c9c20 and material-draw functions in 0x66xxxxx-0x67xxxxx); the VV/RTX-only passes of 0xbb3-0xbcd are simply not entered in Fancy.

## 6ac. GraphicsTasks chain [V strings, call graph by disassembly]
- `0xc267200` (0x632) "GraphicsTasks - Render": the render-thread frame lambda; calls `0xc25fc50` x3 (execute), wait/signal helpers `0xeec5920/0xeec5930/0xeec58f0/0xeec5860/0xeec5870`.
- `0xc25fc50` (0x14a) "GraphicsTasks - Execute rendering tasks and frame": calls `0xc25fe20` x2 (task runner) and **`0xbc65800` (the bgfx API/render-thread handoff, 6r)** once: this is where the encoded frame is handed to the bgfx render side.
- `0xc25f960` (0x256) "Wait for previous frame": calls `0xc25f700` x2 and `0xc25fc50`.
- `0xc25b1a0` (0x960) "GraphicsTasks Scheduler": allocation heavy (0x9bc830 x11, free x5), uses 0x1d2c10 x5, 0x7cd250 x4, `0xc264090` x2 (task queue).
- Frame renderer entry `0x67c9c20` (6ab) helpers: `0xc267b90` (0x154d, frame resource setup: 15 allocs, 14 frees), `0xc270330` (0x124, per-frame teardown: calls 0xbcc1ae0 x2, 0xbc66c50, 0xbc60d10), `0x67ca960` (0x197) and `0x67cab00` (0xce: 7 x `0xa784c0` = the 7 resource-set resets matching the 7 at the start).
Chain: game thread -> FrameBuilder inserts -> `BgfxFrameBuilder::endFrame` (0x66232b0) -> Frame renderer 0x67c9c20 -> BgfxContext::endFrame 0xc270460 (encode graph 0xc273420, tasks 0xe9002e0) -> GraphicsTasks Render 0xc267200 -> Execute 0xc25fc50 -> bgfx handoff 0xbc65800 -> frame() 0xbc66de0 -> RendererContextD3D12::submit 0xe82e230.

## 6ad. Two hash families [V]
- Pass / material names (HashedString): **FNV-1 64** (6p).
- Task-record kinds seen by RenderMarkHook are **FNV-1a 64 of uniform names**: `0x481CF7B16E204CF8` = fnv1a("UseAlphaRewrite") (the actor-body draw tasks: the BatchActor extractor binds the UseAlphaRewrite uniform before each body draw, ~5 per actor), `0x48603E36F5CE3EB2` = fnv1a("LightWorldSpaceDirection"). So a "task kind" is the hash of the uniform the task sets, which explains why kinds are shared by many draws. Uniform-name hashing helper in the extractor: XXH64 0x19932c0 (6u) is for keys; uniform handles are looked up by FNV-1a. To identify more task kinds: hash every uniform name seen in the extractor strings (TintedAlphaTestEnabled, MetalnessUniform, ... 6u) with FNV-1a and compare against kinds observed.

## 6ae. Resource-state tracking (B7b) [I from 0xe82d960 disassembly]
`0xe82d960(tracker rcx, ?, handle r8w (0xFFFF = none), state r9d)` (size 0x359) is the texture state-transition helper used by submit: per-texture tracking entries live in a paged array at `tracker+8` accessed through `0xe83aed0(array, handle)` (entry: byte at +8 = tracked flag, dword at +4 = current D3D12 resource state). If untracked it tail-calls `0xe83b0e0(tracker, handle, state)` (0x11c) which emits the barrier; if tracked and the state differs it stores the new state and (via the 0xB8-byte texture record at `[tracker]+h*0xB8`, 0xe83abb0 guard) updates the record and emits a transition (state value 8 = D3D12_RESOURCE_STATE_RENDER_TARGET? is special-cased: `cmp edi, 8`). `0xe83abb0(table, handle)` is the common bounds/guard check, `0xe83aed0` the paged-array element getter.

### 6af. Draw executor 0xe821120 — prologue and local-state setup (partial, static)
- Entry args: rcx -> [local+0xa0] (bgfx-side block holding the view array: view i record at `[a0 + i*0x140 + 0x310]`, `+0x80/+0x40` sub-blocks), r8 -> [local+0x168] (Frame*), r9 -> [local+0x3a8], r14 = RendererContext (this). Stack frame 0xe238.
- Table base pointers are materialised once from the renderer (confirms 6z): textures `r14+0xBDE178`, `0x93A178`, `0x9B2178`, `0xA32178`, `0xB62178`, `0xC97698`; plus `r14+0x19f90/0x19f94`, `[r14+0x238]`.
- Frame-relative pointers taken: `Frame+0x200` (view matrices), `+0x300`, `+0xB4700`, `+0xE746DC`, `+0xE748C8`; scalar read `Frame+0xDC5814` seeds the per-frame flag word (`[local+0x210]`, bit1 derived by `add cl,cl; and cl,2`).
- Local scratch inits: 0x100-byte 0xFF fills (sort/bind caches at local+0x694, view remap identity table at local+0x798 as uint16[n], n from [local+0x796]), a 0x20-byte block copied from `[local+0x3b0]` result of helper 0xe8360f0 (draw-range batch descriptor: base ptrs +8/+0x10, count +0x1c, kind==2 selects the second stream).
- Status: the 0xc3c3-byte body (state bits → PSO key, binding, draw emission) is NOT yet decoded; A9, B3b-d, B4 stay open. A5b-d and the encoder remain inlined/unlocated.

### 6ag. Predefined uniforms (A7a/A7b)
- `0xbc60100` = `nameToPredefinedUniform(const char*)`: linear strncmp (0xebce570) over a name table at `0x37e1788`-relative (14 entries, 8 bytes each) and returns the enum index; `nullptr` arg path compares the fixed list; returns 0xE when not found.
- Enum order (14 entries; stock bgfx has 15, this fork has no `u_alphaRef` single — it is `u_alphaRef4`): 0 u_viewRect, 1 u_viewTexel, 2 u_view, 3 u_invView, 4 u_proj, 5 u_invProj, 6 u_viewProj, 7 u_invViewProj, 8 u_prevViewProj, 9 u_model, 10 u_modelView, 11 u_modelViewProj, 12 u_prevWorldPosOffset, 13 u_alphaRef4. (`prevViewProj`/`prevWorldPosOffset` are Mojang additions for TAA/motion vectors.)
- Callers: `0xbc75440` (API-side createUniform, 0x67a bytes), and backend-side `0xe801610`, `0xe8137b0`, `0xe859300` (D3D12 program/uniform reflection, 0xc52/0xa22/0xa9d bytes). Registry hash table and per-draw uniform stream layout (A7c) still open.

### 6ah. createUniform 0xbc75440 and the uniform registry (A7a)
- Signature: `UniformHandle* createUniform(Context* ctx /*rcx*/, UniformHandle* out /*rdx*/, const char* name /*r8*/, UniformType type /*r9d*/, uint16_t num /*stack, min 1*/)`. Takes the context lock at `ctx+0x1c0` (acquire `0xebdbf10`, release `0xebdbf20`), returns `out` with 0xFFFF on failure.
- First calls `0xbc60100` (6ag); only non-predefined names (result == 0xE) are registered. Predefined ones return a fixed handle.
- Name hash: `0xbc76610` writes a 16-byte digest; folded with MurmurHash2 mixing (m=0x5bd1e995) to a u32 key. Probe start `((h*0x5ebca77)>>0x13)*0x1b1 & 0x3ff`; table = 1024 slots, linear probing, wrap `&0x3ff`.
- Hash table: keys `u32 @ ctx+0x1E1C2A4[1024]`, handles `u16 @ ctx+0x1E1D2A4[1024]` (0xFFFF = empty).
- Handle allocator: count `u16 @ ctx+0x1DFB690`, max `u16 @ +0x1DFB692`, dense list `u16[] @ +0x1DFB694` (the same dense/sparse scheme as the other handle allocators in 6s).
- Uniform records: stride 0x18 at `ctx+0x1E1DAA8`: `+0` name data pointer (owned string, freed via the allocator vtable slot 1 on reuse), `+8` name length (int32), `+0x10` int32 uniform type (indexes the size table at rva 0x37cc212-based `rdx+type*4`), `+0x14` u16 array count.
- Re-creating an existing name returns the existing handle and keeps the larger of (type size, count); a smaller-or-equal request is a no-op, a larger one re-registers (shows `cmp r8d,[rdx+r9*4]` size compare at 0xbc7572e).

Every one of these touches `ctx+0x1CFD0C8` (the API-thread command/lock state) and the queue-of-destroys at `Frame-like +0xDC5818/0xDC5820` (deferred destroy list: begin/end of the per-frame release vector). The deferred destroy queue is drained by `frame()` (6s).

Takeaways: `this+0x19F08..0x19F50` hold device/queue/factory COM pointers (init and shutdown); `0x19F58` = bgfx Context, `0x19F60/0x19F68` = Caps/shader-format info; `0x19F80..0x19F94` = swapchain/resolution state; `0x19EE0..0x19EF0`, `0x19FDD` are submit state (current frame/flags).

### 6ak. TDR / device-lost and init facts (B5, B8b) [V strings]
- Slot 7 `0xe8102b0` (0x1924 bytes) is the **device-lost / TDR handler**, not device creation: strings "Invalid Shader. FAILED 0x%08x", "Failed to create resource. FAILED 0x%08x", "Unable Initialize. FAILED 0x%08x", DRED report ("Ops", "UNKNOWN OP", "PageFaultVA %I64x", "DRED Data unavailable hr = "), HRESULT name table (DXGI_ERROR_DEVICE_REMOVED/RESET/HUNG/DRIVER_INTERNAL_ERROR/NOT_CURRENTLY_AVAILABLE), final "Device Lost FAILED 0x%08x %s (%s)". Reads queue `0xCC9268` and fields `0xC9C6D0`, `0x19F85..8C`. Called from submit on failure (the `_initiateTDR` path).
- Slot 58 `0xe807ff0` init: dynamic loads kernel32 `CreateEventExA` (fence events) and d3d12.dll `D3D12CreateDevice`, `D3D12EnableExperimentalFeatures`, `D3D12GetDebugInterface`, `D3D12SerializeRootSignature` (**root signature is built at init and serialized in code, not read from a file**); stores device/queue/factory COM pointers at `this+0x19F08..0x19F50`; version string "%hu.%hu.%hu.%hu" = driver version read for the vendor workaround switch.
- Slot 59 `0xe80dec0` shutdown/reset touches the same 0x19F08 block; only the guarded-array string.
- Open: exact root parameter layout, descriptor heap sizes and PSO key remain inside submit (0xe82e230) — need the live root signature blob (capture serialized blob via a breakpoint at the D3D12SerializeRootSignature call 0xe808108-region, then parse).
