### 6cn. Tag 7 material hash resolved live: `ender_dragon.skinning`; material-name node layout
Pause-and-read capture at the tag-7 handler (3 hits, identical): `[rdi+0x140]` = P (0x4BE197C5BF0, a **material-name node**), layout: `+0x00/+0x08` two pointers (list links / begin-end; P+0 points back to the node area), **`+0x10` u64 = FNV-1 64 hash `0x0694BEA7E37055A4`**, **`+0x18` char* -> "ender_dragon.skinning"**, `+0x20` u64 length = 0x15 (21), `+0x28` u64 capacity = 0x1F (so it is a std::string-like {ptr, size, capacity} stored right after the hash: a `HashedString` {hash, string} with heap storage), `+0x30..` a pointer to the owning material record and flags. FNV-1 64 of "ender_dragon.skinning" verified against the captured hash. So tag 7 in this live scene is the **Ender Dragon skinned model** (the player is in the End), proving that tag 7 handles skinned entity models (name pattern `<entity material>.skinning`), not only the block-entity overlays seen in the hash-switch constants (6cj): the BST leaves cover cracks/selection overlays, other names fall through to the generic path via the resolver 0x675e440. Material hook point confirmed: the HashedString at `payload+0x140` (hash at +0x10 of the node it points to) is the per-item material identity the visitor switches on.
Also confirmed here: in-End scene explains the tag mix (dragon skinned mesh, end sky) seen so far.

### 6co. LIVE ShaderBuffer writes from the terrain extractor (0x5679db0 hardware breakpoint, pause-and-read)
Harness: Lua `debugger_onBreakpoint` on `base+0x5679db0` reading `slot = u16 [RDX]`, span `{count=[R8], ptr=[R8+8]}`, caller = `[RSP]`; filtered to callers inside the terrain extractor (rva 0x66ea3d0..0x66fb000), 3000 breakpoint hits to catch 4 unique (slot, call-site) writes (the extractor runs ~8x/frame, the other 99% of hits come from UI/fullscreen passes, e.g. function **0x666aca0** (0x6c7c bytes; strings Flipbook, UI/Base, CameraAimAssistHighlight, fullscreen_cube_overlay) whose slot-0 write `(0.5938, 0.625, 0.1, 0)` hits every frame).
Live values (scene: The End, player standing, game time ~864 s):
- call site 0x66eb6b5 (ret +0x66eb6ba), **slot 7 = `MeshContext`** (RenderChunk list, 6cc) = `(0, 0, 0, 0)` (chunk/mesh origin offset, zero for the camera-relative base case);
- call site 0x66eb901 (ret +0x66eb906), **slot 10 = `ViewPositionAndTime`** = `(-0, 0, -0, 863.9)`: xyz = view position (camera-relative rendering, so zero) and **w = elapsed time in seconds** (863.9 matches the session uptime; drives water/portal/UV animation);
- call site 0x66eb998 (ret +0x66eb99d), **slot 2 = `GlobalRoughness`** (RenderChunk list) = `(0.6563, 1.0, 128.0, 57.0)`: global PBR/roughness-style parameter vector for the chunk pass (not the fog vector: `FogAndDistanceControl` is slot 3 and is written by another call site not caught in the window);
- call site 0x66eba35 (ret +0x66eba3a), **slot 4** = `(0.0402, 0.0494, 0.09, 1.0)`: dark blue-violet = End fog colour (`FogColor`).
Consistency check: all four captured (slot, name) pairs (7 MeshContext, 10 ViewPositionAndTime, 2 GlobalRoughness, 4 FogColor) agree with the base `RenderChunk` uniform file order of 6cc, while `RenderChunkPrepass` (7 uniforms, ViewPositionAndTime at index 6) and `RenderChunkForwardPBR` (82 uniforms) do not, so the live variant in this scene (End, non-PBR path) is the base `RenderChunk` material and **slot id = index in the file's uniform list** holds. This proves the write path end-to-end: material uniform list -> slot id -> 0x5679db0 -> vec4 span in the extractor's local buffer.

### 6cp. Live frame reads (2026-11-12) and the multi-threaded draw executor job (0xe83c390 -> 0xe821120)
Live CE reads: bgfx::Context ptr `[base+0x12ae1748]` = 0x4BDF6010040; API frame `[ctx+0x1CFD0C0]` = 0x4BDF6023D80, submit frame `[ctx+0x1CFD0C8]` = 0x4BDF6E98740 (distance 0xE749C0 = Frame.h kFrameSize, validated live); **frame counter at +0xE746D8 = 23091 / 23092 on the two frames (consecutive, increments by 1 per frame: kFrameCounter [V] confirmed)**; header +0x20 live values seen in headers `0x0100005000000027` (x8), `0x010000500000001F` (x2), `0x0100001000000017` (x1) (state word upper bits `0x01000050`/`0x01000010`, lower `0x27/0x1F/0x17`, i.e. a small family of states: the low byte varies 0x17/0x1F/0x27 = bit 3 and bit 5 steps) and `+0x28` = 0 in all samples. `+0xE746C8/+0xE746D0` are **0 at the submit entry on both frames**, so that u64 pair is not an item count (it is a per-frame statistics pair set later by submit; Frame.h/6e comments "counter + capacity" should be read as statistics). The real item count is not stored in the Frame header area read here: the sorted list length is carried in the executor job ranges (below).
**Draw executor job 0xe83c390 (0xd0 bytes)** is the job lambda `operator()(job*, int jobIndex)`: it reads the shared job state `r8 = job->[+8]` = {ptr0 -> submit-side context (`rdx`), ptr1 -> **vector of per-job [begin,end) offsets into the sorted item list** (`r11[idx]` and `r11[idx+1]`, u64 each, so N jobs have N+1 boundary entries)}, picks the job's **command list from `this->[+0x19F60][jobIndex]`** (`mov rcx,[rdx+0x19f60]; mov rcx,[rcx+idx*8]`), copies a small capture struct and calls the executor **0xe821120(cmdList, ?, ...)** with the slice `[begin,end)`; the executor (frame 0xe238 bytes, ~0x1d000 code bytes) then walks `sorted[begin..end)` as in 6am. So the **draw encode is parallel over job slices with one D3D12 command list per job** (array at this+0x19F60), and `this+0x19F58` is the primary/current list (6av). Consequence for hooks/C8: per-draw hooks run on several worker threads concurrently; any stateful hook needs per-job storage. Executor prologue: `[rbp+0xe228]` is the 5th argument (capture struct) whose `+0x38` object's vcall `+0x10` fills `rbx+0x5f0` (a 0x18-byte begin/end/flags result, `[rbx+0x608]==1` = ok), then `esi = u16 [rbx+0x5f0]` (job's view/queue id), `r15d = [this+0xc9c6d0]` (back-buffer/swapchain index), clears `rdi+0x11c8` (0x10 bytes) and `rdi+0x1140` (0x88 bytes) (per-job statistics).

### 6cq. bgfx Encoder::submit located: 0xbc5fb40 (rva) [V static + live-consistent]
Found by scanning code for the Frame offsets 0x94700/0xB4700 (17 hits: encoder submit 0xbc5fb40..0xbc61xxx four copies = submit variants (submit/submitOcclusion/dispatch/blit-ish at 0xbc6079d, 0xbc60b52, 0xbc60f4f), and the executor 0xe820c53/0xe8216d4/0xe821815/0xe821a75 and 0xe7f51d2/0xe7f5469/0xe85e5b8/0xe85e873 readers). So the encoder IS a separate (non-inlined) function after all; A5b-d are mapped here.
**0xbc5fb40 = Encoder::submit(Encoder* this=rcx, u16 viewId=dx, u16 program=r8w (cmove 0xffff->0), r9=depth, stack: u32 depth/seq at [rsp+0xc0], u8 preserve-state at [rsp+0xc8])**. Encoder object fields (`rsi`): `[+0]` Frame*, `+0x08` u32 depth/sequence value, `+0x0C` u32 sequence counter result, `+0x10` u16 program, `+0x12` u16 viewId, `+0x14` u32 (low 2 bits = view/pass flag), `+0x40..+0x7F` the draw header being built (copied to Frame draw header at +0xB4700+idx*0x80; **+0x60 = u64 state, default 0x010000500000001F (movabs at 0xbc5ff6e, written back after each submit when not preserving)**, +0x68 state2, +0x70.. transform block, +0x80/+0x90 scissor/stencil defaults 0x100000000, 0x1ffff00000000), `+0x74/+0x8c` ..., `+0x84/+0x8c` (non-zero = has draw data; if both 0 the submit is discarded: `inc [+0x690]`), `+0x140..+0x653` RenderBind (0x514 bytes, hashed with MurmurHash2 `0x5bd1e995` over 4 words -> `+0x654` bind hash; `+0x688` dirty flag), `+0x680` ptr non-null -> post-submit callback `0xbc60310(Frame, idx, ptr)`, `+0x68c` submitted counter, `+0x690` discarded counter, `+0x694/+0x698` per-view block values, `+0x69c..` u32[] per-stage values (min of them stored at +0x8c), `+0x6ac` u8 view slot, `+0x6ad` u8 1 = encoder discarded (tail-jump to 0xbc60110 which resets).
**Draw index allocation:** atomic CAS increment of **u32 `Frame+0xDC57E8` (the draw/item count)** with cap 0xFFFF (maxDrawCalls 65535: `cmp 0xFFFE; jb ok; else discard`). This is the item count I could not find before: **live read `[SubmitFrame+0xDC57E8]`**. Per-view block table `[Frame+0xDC57E0][viewSlot]` (u32 at +4 = view's running index copied to Encoder+0x698).
**Arrays indexed by idx (edi):** sort keys u64 `Frame+0x14700 + idx*8`; sorted index list at `Frame+0x94700` (written `[Frame+idx*2+0x94700]` in the "all-in" variant, so u16 entries; confirmed 65535 entries -> 0x14700+0x7FFF8 = 0x94700 exact); draw header 0x80 `Frame+0xB4700 + idx*0x80` (movaps x8 from encoder+0x00..+0x7F: header = encoder+0x00..+0x7F verbatim, so **header+0x20 state == Encoder+0x60?** the copy starts at Encoder+0x40 for the last 4 xmm: header+0x00..0x3F = Encoder+0x40..0x7F? NO: rbx = Encoder+0x40, header+0x70 = rbx+0x70-... see below); full RenderDraw 0x540 bytes copied to `[Frame+0x8B4700] + idx*0x540` via `0xeec62c0(dst, Encoder+0x140, 0x518)` (memcpy of the RenderBind 0x518 bytes; `lea rcx,[r15+0x8b4700]` + `call 0xbc79bc0` grows/commits that array to idx).
Header mapping (verified by the movaps pairs: header+0x70 = rbx+0x70... i.e. header offset k = Encoder offset 0x40+k for k in 0..0x7F? the code uses `[rbx+k]` -> `[header+0xb4700+k]`, so header[k] = Encoder[0x40+k]): header+0x20 = Encoder+0x60 = u64 draw state (live: 0x0100005000000027 / 1F / 17 -> default 0x1F), header+0x28 = Encoder+0x68 state2 (0), header+0x30..+0x3F transform/scissor block = Encoder+0x70/+0x80, header+0x60.. = Encoder+0xA0.. (viewId/slot fields written at +0xA4 (view slot byte), +0xA3 flag, +0x74 u64 = [+0x694], +0xBA u16 occlusion query id, +0x67 |= 0x40 when occlusion query set).
**Sort key construction (three modes selected by per-view mode byte table `[global 0x6e7fabc]+viewId*0x140+0x1eab1b2` = 1 Sequence, 2 DepthAscending, 3 DepthDescending (neg depth), else Default/program-state):**
- mode 1 (Sequence): `key = (seq&0xFFFFF)<<30 | (program<<17)&0xFFE0000 | (flags&3)<<28 | viewId<<54 | 0x18<<48`; seq from `lock xadd [global+viewId*4+0x1eaac60]` (per-view sequence counter) also stored to Encoder+0xC.
- modes 2/3 (Depth): `key = depth<<18 | (program<<5)&... | (flags&3)<<16 | viewId<<54 | 0x14<<48`.
- default: `key = depth<<5 | (program&0x7FF)<<37 | (flags&3)<<48 | viewId<<54 | 0x10<<48`.
So the top 10 bits of the 64-bit sort key are the **viewId (54..63)**, bits 48-53 the mode tag (0x10/0x14/0x18), and the 24-bit field `seq`/`depth` below. This is the encode-order key the sorted list `Frame+0x94700` is built from (radix sort by 0xe7f.. / 0xe85e..). Hook points for all rendering: `0xbc5fb40` (submit, args above), `0xbc60110` (discard/reset).

### 6cr. Encoder submit variants, reset and Frame sort (0xbc60110..0xbc61440) [V static]
Function map of the encoder block (all take Encoder* in rcx, all start with `cmp byte [rcx+0x6ad],1` -> discard path `jmp 0xbc60110`, all allocate the draw index by the same CAS on `Frame+0xDC57E8`, all write key to `Frame+idx*8+0x14700`, sorted entry (u16, the draw's own idx initially) to `Frame+idx*2+0x94700`, copy 0x80 header to `+0xB4700`, call `0xbc79bc0` (commit the 0x540 RenderDraw array to idx), `0xeec62c0` (memcpy 0x518 RenderBind), `0xbc60310` (post-submit callback when `Encoder+0x680`!=0)):
- `0xbc5fb40` submit(view, program, depth, flags, preserveState) - the normal draw (6cq).
- `0xbc60540` submit variant with one more stack arg (`ecx=[rsp+0xc8]` kept as a u32 instead of the preserve byte): view/program/depth and the same three key modes minus the occlusion-query handling; writes `Encoder+0xC` from `edx`. Used for the occlusion-query / indirect-count form (calls `0xeec62d0` (memcpy variant) and a COM-thunk `[0x3d3e699]` = a D3D resource-state helper).
- `0xbc60960` **no key args: dispatch-style submit** - `program=0`, `Encoder+0x10=0`, key is the constant `0x0020000000000000` (tag 0x20<<48, plus view in bits 54+ applied by caller: `rcx` movabs) => compute/blit items sort in their own band after draws of the same view.
- `0xbc60ce0` submit variant with `Encoder+0x10 = 0xffff` (no program) and two stack args (`[rsp+0xd0]`, `[rsp+0xd8]`): the **indirect/blit-like** form: key = `rax` built from viewId/stack args, no mode switch.
- `0xbc60110` **Encoder::reset(this)**: clears `+0x6ad`; restores defaults: `+0x60` state = 0x010000500000001F, `+0x68`=0, `+0x70` zero block, `+0x80` constant 16-byte (default rect/transform from rodata `[rip+0x342de44]`), `+0x90`=0x100000000, `+0x98`=0x1ffff00000000, `+0xA0`=0xFFFF (u16), `+0xA2`=0, `+0xA3`=0xFF00 (u16: flag byte + 0), `+0x40`=0xffffffff00000000, `+0xB4`=-1 (u64), `+0xA8`=0x7fffffff (int, depth/clear value), `+0xAC..+0xE3` zeroed/0xFFFF-filled stencil/stream defaults (`+0xCC`=0xFFFF, `+0xDC`=0xFFFF0000, `+0xE2`=0xFF); then clears the RenderBind block `+0x140..` (loop with offset 0x167: `0xffff` slot handles, `0xffffffff` buffer handles) and calls the RenderBind release through `[ptr+0x...]` COM-style vtable when `+0x680` set.
- `0xbc61220` **Frame::sort** (calls `0xbc61440` radix sort with `r8 = Frame+0x94700` (sorted u16 index list), `rcx = Frame+0x14700` (u64 keys)). Before the sort it patches per-view boundary keys: keys of per-view "begin/end" markers are rewritten with mask `0xC03FFFFFFFFFFFFF` (clears key bits 54..61 = viewId low 8 bits) so the sort places view boundaries correctly; after the sort the executor walks `sorted[]`. 0xe9e6d70 and 0xebcd830 are the task-system dispatch helpers used to run the sort in parallel jobs.
Hook consequence: swapping/draw-skipping can be done at `Encoder::submit` (0xbc5fb40/0xbc60540) before the draw is recorded (view/program available as args, state at `Encoder+0x60`, binds at `+0x140`), or by rewriting a key after submit; `bgfx::setState` is not a call (it writes `Encoder+0x60` directly = inlined at call sites), so state hooks must read `Encoder+0x60` at submit time.

### 6cs. Executor per-item loop and header fields (0xe821120 loop at 0xe8216c0..0xe829090) [V static]
The item loop in the executor takes the sorted index `idx = u16 [Frame(rbx+0x168)+pos*2+0x94700]` (three variants of the load at 0xe8216d2/0xe821814/0xe821a73: draw item, "skip/empty" item, and the third item class), then: `RenderDraw* d = [Frame+0x8B4700] + idx*0x540` (`imul 0x540`), `[Frame+0x8B4778+idx*8]` = per-draw u64 loaded into `rbx+0x110` (resource-set / bind key; the 8-byte table after the 0x540 array header) and `u32 [d+0x514]` into `rbx+0x178` (the bind hash = Encoder+0x654 copy, i.e. RenderBind.hash at +0x514 of the 0x540 record, so the 0x518-byte memcpy of 6cr copies RenderBind 0x514 + hash). Draw header `h = [rbx+0x590] + idx*0x80` (rbx+0x590 = Frame+0xB4700 base).
Header byte **`h+0x23` is the item kind** (low u32 of state at +0x20 bits 24..31): `0` = regular item (draw/dispatch handled by the jump at 0xe8217fb -> 0xe828f50), `1` = item executed through the command-list object's vtable slot +0x200 with the extra args `[rbx+0x658],[rbx+0x660],[rbx+0x1f0]` (the draw record) and `[rbx+0x90a0]+(rbx+0x1e8)<<6` (a 0x40-byte per-draw constant slot): this is the ray tracing / indirect-compute path. Regular path (0xe828fea): `h+0x0C` u16 = **index buffer handle** (0xFFFF = non-indexed): non-indexed -> cmdlist vtbl `+0x70` with `(startVertex=[h+0x10], instanceCount=[h+0x14], ...=[h+0x18])` i.e. DrawInstanced-style args `{u32 [+0x10], u32 [+0x14], u32 [+0x18]}`; indexed -> `[rbx+0x588]+handle*0x80` = index buffer record, `numIndices = u16 [h+0x1E]` (0xFFFF = whole buffer, `record[+0x60]>>5` = size/32?), `startIndex = u16 [h+0x1C]<<5`, base vertex from `[Frame+0x19f40]`, cmdlist vtbl `+0x1D8` (DrawIndexedInstanced-style), with xmm9 = instance/base pair. The per-draw program change test is `u16 [rbx+rsi*4+0x696]` linked-list of program slots (LRU list of resource-sets; `[rbx+0x10c]` = current program slot, set via vtbl `+0x100`).
Stats: `lock inc [ctx+0x4c]` (draw counter) at each item start, so a live read of `[[rbx+0x3a8]+0x4c]` after the frame gives the executed item count per job.
The blend/depth/cull bits of the u64 `h+0x20` are consumed by the PSO-key builder reached from the jump at 0xe8217fb; its bit map is still to do (listed as B4/A9 in the checklist).

### 6ct. Draw-state u64 bit map from the D3D12 PSO builder 0xe81dc60 [V static; field meanings by table lookups, enum values not yet dumped]
`0xe81dc60(ctx=rcx, ?, frame=rdx, state=r13?, state2=r9, ...)` is the **graphics PSO create/lookup**: it decodes the header u64 state `S` (header+0x20, = Encoder+0x60) and `state2` (header+0x28) into a D3D12_GRAPHICS_PIPELINE_STATE_DESC, hashes it, and looks it up/creates in the PSO cache. Called from the executor item loop at 0xe82213f with `r9 = [h+0x28]`, `[h+0x50]`, `[h+0x58]`, `[h+0x63]` (vertex stream mask byte, 0xFF = none; stream i uses `u16 [h+0x4+i*8], [h+0x6+i*8]` (vertex buffer handle / layout handle per stream) and fills `[rbx+0x2c0+i*8]` with `layoutTable+handle*0x54`), `[h+0x68]`, `[h+0x6c]`, `[h+0x70]` (float) etc.
**PSO key = `S & 0x0707007FFFFFF0FF`, `state2 & 0xFFFFFF00FFFFFF00`** (these bits are the only ones that change the PSO; everything else is dynamic state): kept bits 0-7, 12-38, 48-50, 56-58 of S; stencil ref/mask low bytes of each state2 half are removed.
Bit map of **S** (shifts read from the decode, fields verified, enum value tables at the lea targets (rva 0xe81e55x `ccd68f`, `ccd511`, `ccd365`, `ccd3db`) not yet dumped):
- bits 0-3: color write mask (RGBA; `and al,0xf` -> RenderTarget write mask). Default 0xF; 0x7 = alpha write off.
- bits 4-7: **depth test function** (4 bits; 0 = depth disabled: `setne` -> DepthEnable; value indexes a 16-entry D3D12 comparison table at rva 0xccd365). Default 1 (LESS), 0x27 has 2.
- bits 9..: (bits 8-11 are NOT in the PSO key: per-draw, cleared by the mask).
- bits 12-15, 16-19, 20-23, 24-27: four 4-bit blend factors (indexed into the 16-entry blend factor table 0xccd68f via `(S>>9)&0x78` etc.): srcRGB, dstRGB, srcAlpha, dstAlpha (bgfx order); `S & 0x0FFFF000 != 0` -> BlendEnable.
- bits 28-30 (`(S>>0x1a)&0x1c`, 3 bits): blend equation index (table 0xccd511? 4-byte entries).
- bits 31-33 (`(S>>0x1f)&7`): 3-bit field stored raw in the desc (candidate: sample/MSAA or topology); bit 34 (`>>0x22 &1`) and bit 35 (`>>0x23 &1`) flags stored raw in the desc (alpha-to-coverage / independent blend candidates).
- bits 36-37 (`(S>>0x24)&3`): **cull mode** -> table 0xccd511 -> D3D12_CULL_MODE (default 1 in 0x0100005000000027: bits 36-37 = 1? value 0x5 at bits 32-39 => bit 34 set (0x4), bit 36 set (0x10) -> cull 1, flag bit 34).
- bit 38 (`>>0x26 &1`): depth-write flag (stored in desc) .
- bits 48-50: 3-bit field (kept in key; executor also tests `0x0007000000000000` for per-state-change detection): likely primitive topology type / fill mode.
- bit 56 / 57 / 58: three single-bit flags stored raw (default has bit 56 set: 0x01000050...): candidates conservative raster / depth clip / line AA.
- bit 62 (`0x4000000000000000`, tested on `rbx+0x1f0` = draw record flags in the executor): "dirty/force reapply" for bind state.
**state2 = stencil**: two 32-bit halves (low = front face, high = back face; if high == 0 the front is reused via `cmove`): per half: `>>0x12 &0x3c`, `>>0x16 &0x3c`, `>>0x1c`, `>>0xe &0x3c` index tables 0xccd3db (stencil op) / 0xccd365 (comparison func) -> fail / depth-fail / pass op and func; byte 1 of low half (bits 8-15) = stencil read mask `[rsp+5c8]`, write mask 0xFF fixed; stencil ref byte in bits 0-7 (and 32-39) is excluded from the PSO key. `state2 != 0` -> StencilEnable.
Practical use for hooks: state bits for no-depth-test (bits 4-7 = 0), additive/alpha blend (bits 12-27, 28-30), cull (36-37) and color mask (0-3) can be rewritten in `Encoder+0x60` at submit time (0xbc5fb40 args) or in the header before the executor reads it; changing key bits creates a new PSO on the render thread (first-use compile cost).

### 6cu. Enum tables behind the draw-state bits (values dumped from .rdata) [V]
Tables read from the exe (rva = lea site end + disp): 
- **Compare func (depth and stencil), rva 0xf4ebbb0**, indexed by the 4-bit field: `[0]=0 (disabled), 1->2 LESS, 2->4 LESS_EQUAL, 3->3 EQUAL, 4->7 GREATER_EQUAL, 5->5 GREATER, 6->6 NOT_EQUAL, 7->1 NEVER, 8->8 ALWAYS` (D3D12_COMPARISON_FUNC values; bgfx order LESS, LEQUAL, EQUAL, GEQUAL, GREATER, NOTEQUAL, NEVER, ALWAYS). So S bits 4-7 = depth func with that code, default 1 = LESS, 0x27's 2 = LEQUAL.
- **Blend factor, rva 0xf4ebbe0**, 8-byte pairs {rgbFactor, alphaFactor} indexed by the 4-bit field: `0:(0,0) none, 1:(1,1) ZERO, 2:(2,2) ONE, 3:(3,5) SRC_COLOR (alpha uses SRC_ALPHA), 4:(4,6) INV_SRC_COLOR (alpha INV_SRC_ALPHA), 5:(5,5) SRC_ALPHA, 6:(6,6) INV_SRC_ALPHA, 7:(7,7) DEST_ALPHA, 8:(8,8) INV_DEST_ALPHA` (D3D12_BLEND values; more entries follow for DEST_COLOR etc.). S bits 12-15 = src, 16-19 = dst (RGB), 20-23 / 24-27 = src/dst alpha fields (the alpha channel uses the same table's second column when the combined-field form is used).
- **Cull, rva 0xf4ebc90** `{1,2,3,0}` indexed by S bits 36-37: 0 -> D3D12_CULL_NONE(1), 1 -> FRONT(2), 2 -> BACK(3), 3 -> 0 (invalid). Default state 0x...27 has bits 36-37 = 1 (front-face cull); 0 = no culling.
- **Stencil op, rva 0xf4ebc70** `{2,1,3,7,4,8,5,6}` indexed by the 3-bit op field: ZERO->2(ZERO), KEEP->1, REPLACE->3, INCR->7, INCR_SAT->4, DECR->8, DECR_SAT->5, INVERT->6 (bgfx order ZERO, KEEP, REPLACE, INCR, INCRSAT, DECR, DECRSAT, INVERT). Stencil state2 halves: func bits 14-17 (compare table above), fail op bits 18-21, depth-fail op bits 22-25, pass op bits 28-31 (as decoded in 6ct).

### 6cv. Item payload field decode from the live dumps (docs/data/live_item_payloads_1.26.62.01.txt) [V live data, field meanings inferred]
Decoded as floats/pointers (pointers are heap `0x000004BE........`/`0x00007FF7........`; floats seen in-scene with the local player around world (2.4, 0.88, -1.35) and an ender-dragon model at (60.5, 0.69, 11.5)):
- **tag 7 (skinned actor model item; first sample ender_dragon.skinning)**: `+0x00..+0x3F` 4x4 float **model matrix (row-vector, rows of 16 B): rotation*uniform scale 0.008839 (=1/113.1), translation row at +0x30 = (60.5, 0.6878, 11.5, 1)** i.e. the actor's world position; `+0x40..+0x7F` the **same matrix again = previous-frame matrix** (equal here since the entity is static; used for motion vectors); `+0x80..+0x97` six floats (-12,-12,-24,12,24,48) = **local-space AABB min/max** followed by (3.623, FLT_MAX, FLT_MAX, -FLT_MAX, -FLT_MAX: a second bounding/range record with sentinels at +0x98..+0xAF); `+0xB0..+0xFF` mixed pointer pairs (skin matrix / bone buffers `0x4BE65CDCC30`, `...65C96E00`, `...31E455C0`: vector begin/end pairs, 0x8 flags), `+0x100..+0x117` floats (0.7788, 0.8854 ...) = UV/animation parameters, `+0x118` (14.34, 22.51) = distance/size pair, `+0x140` = **HashedString node* pair (material name) then +0x150 `0x00030000`** (3 = material/blend class, bits 16-17), `+0x154..+0x15F` three 1.0 floats (tint / scale) + 1.0, `+0x164` u32 0x14db20 (handle). 
- **tag 1**: starts with a pointer pair, `+0x08..+0x17` floats (0.4903, ..., 0.8868), `+0x18/+0x28` = position triple (0.003129, -0.02643, ...) = mesh-local offsets, `+0x30` = (-0.01279, 0, 2.422, 0.88) and `+0x40` = (-1.349, 1, 2.422, 0.88): **world position of the drawn actor in +0x38..+0x4C (x=2.422, y=0.88, z=-1.349 = the local player/camera-adjacent)**, `+0x58..` pointers, `+0x7C` = 0.25 (scale), then `+0x88..+0x97` = `FFFFFFFF FFFFFFFF FFFFFFFE FFFFFF00` = "none" sentinels (invalid handles, -2 index), `+0xD8..+0x13F` a **copy of the same matrix block** (previous-frame set again) followed by tint floats 0.0579 and `3f01c056` / (-2.548, 3.284) = UV scale.
- **tag 0**: `+0x00..+0x1F` four heap pointers (vectors), `+0x18..+0x6F` a 3x4 transform + constants (0.0129, 0.0233, ... then (1.945, 0.88), (-2.213, 1), (2.414, 0.877), (-1.345,1)) -> contains the same position (2.414, 0.877, -1.345) at +0x58 and +0x160; `+0x80 = 0x00000100` flags, `+0x84..+0xAF` colours (0.6686, 0.601, 0.6167, 1.0, 0.5, 0.9375), `+0xC0..+0xCF` (1.0, 0.65625, 1.0, 128.0) = **light/brightness and texture-size constants**, `+0x140..+0x1BF` heap pointer array (stream/buffer handles).
Common pattern: items are 0x200-byte structs whose first 0x80 bytes are the transform pair (current + previous) and whose `+0x140` is the material identity (tag 7); tags 0/1 embed the actor world position twice (visible as the same float triple at two offsets), so an ESP/chams hook can read `pos` straight from the payload without touching Actor. Offsets are good enough to read the transform (tag 7: +0x30 translation row), not yet to name every field.

### 6cw. VERSION CORRECTION for 6cp-6cu (important)
Sections 6cp-6cu were disassembled from **1.26.60.05** (the tool default had been set to it), but the running/target game is **1.26.62.01** (pid check: `...\Instances\1.26.62.01\Minecraft.Windows.exe`). The tool defaults (`tools/sigs/xref.py`, `where.py`, `tools/update_day.py`) are now back on 1.26.62.01. The code is the same, shifted: in this region **all RVAs are +0xC00** in 1.26.62.01, Frame/Encoder field offsets are identical (the 0xB4700/0x94700/0xDC57E8 scans hit the same instruction patterns). Verified 1.26.62.01 RVAs: **Encoder::submit 0xbc60740**, variants 0xbc61140 / 0xbc61560 / 0xbc618e0, **Encoder::reset 0xbc60d10**, **Frame::sort 0xbc61e20**, **executor 0xe821d20** (item loads of u16 sorted at 0xe8222d4/0xe822415/0xe822675), **submit slot 50 0xe82ee30**, **PSO builder 0xe81e860** (contains the 0x0707007FFFFFF0FF key mask), key-mask and all bit maps unchanged. The draw-executor wrapper (6cp, 0xe83c390 in 60.05) is not at +0xC00 (0xe83cf90 is a different function) and must be re-located in 62.01 before use. Anything earlier than 6cp that quotes RVAs was checked live in 62.01 (6ck-6co) or should be re-verified with `where.py` before building a signature from it.

**6cw addendum (checked after the first correction):** the executor ENTRY, the draw-executor wrapper and submit slot 50 are NOT shifted in 1.26.62.01: `0xe821120` (executor, called from the wrapper `0xe83c390` whose bytes and `call 0xe821120` are identical to 60.05), `0xe82e230` (slot 50). The earlier "+0xC00 = 0xe821d20 / 0xe82ee30" matches were prologue-byte false positives; ignore them. Inside the executor the item-loop instructions moved by +0xC02 (sorted-index loads at 0xe8222d4 / 0xe822415 / 0xe822675 instead of 0xe8216d2 / 0xe821814 / 0xe821a73), so for the 6cs addresses add 0xC02 (an extra ~0xC00 bytes were inserted early in the function). Shifted by +0xC00 and verified by distinctive content: Encoder::submit family 0xbc60740 / 0xbc61140 / 0xbc61560 / 0xbc618e0, reset 0xbc60d10, Frame::sort 0xbc61e20, PSO builder 0xe81e860. Rule: verify any 60.05-derived RVA by its content (a mask constant, an offset scan) in 62.01, never by prologue bytes alone.

### 6cx. endFrame arguments and the task dispatch 0xe9002e0 (C4a/C7c start) [V static, 62.01]
`BgfxContext::endFrame` 0xc270460 (`this`=rcx, `rdx`=DragonFrameResources&, `r8`=Declarator&, `r9`=IntraFrameAllocator&, stack `[rsp+...]`=vector<HashedString>&& passed on): early out `cmp byte [this+0x35],0`; `this+0x38` (u64), `this+0x40/+0x48` (swapped 64-bit pair), `this+0x50` and `r14 = IntraFrameAllocator*` are packed in a 0x28-byte struct and passed with `r8=Declarator`, `r9=name vector` to the frame-graph generator **0xc273420(struct*, out*, Declarator, names)**. Then the dispatch struct `{+0x00 IntraFrameAllocator*, +0x08 [this+0x20]+0x118 (pointer to a sub-object), +0x10 &this->[+0x58], +0x18 [this+0x18], +0x20 DragonFrameResources*}` goes to **0xe9002e0(taskList* = out of 0xc273420, args*)**.
**0xe9002e0** walks a `std::vector<Entry>` with **Entry size 0x80** (begin `[rcx]`, end `[rcx+8]`; `[rcx+0x18]` byte = state, 1 -> early path): per entry `Entry+0x00` task object* (virtual `+0x18` returns the task kind; kind 1 = the "owned/registered" kind), `Entry+0x08` shared_ptr control block (refcount `lock inc [cb+8]`), `+0x10..+0x2F` and `+0x30..+0x4F`, `+0x50..+0x6F`, `+0x70`, `+0x78` moved fields (the code move-constructs the entry into a new 0x88-byte object: `operator new(0x88)` through the global allocator vtbl +8). For kind==1 entries it **appends `{object*, control block}` (16 bytes) to a vector in DragonFrameResources: `DragonFrameResources+0x298` = begin, `+0x2A0` = end (capacity slot follows)**, i.e. DragonFrameResources owns the live task/pass objects of the frame (shared_ptr<Task> list). The per-entry index `si` is also pushed into a `vector<u16>` (the run order) at `rbp+0x110..0x120`.
So: **DragonFrameResources layout (known part): `+0x298` begin / `+0x2A0` end of `vector<shared_ptr<Task>>`**; the rest of the object is not touched by endFrame itself; IntraFrameAllocator is the arena used by 0xc273420 (`0x9bc830` push helper x42) and freed through `0xe935700`.

### 6cy. 0xc273420 prologue: argument roles and the IntraFrameAllocator (C4b, C7a) [V static, 62.01]
`0xc273420(S*=rcx, out*=rdx, Declarator*=r8, names*=r9)`: frame 0x35a8 bytes (`__chkstk` via 0xe9e7970), saves xmm6-15. `S` is the 0x28-byte struct built by endFrame (6cx): `S+0x00 this+0x38`, `S+0x08/+0x10` the swapped pair, `S+0x18 this+0x50`, **`S+0x20 = IntraFrameAllocator*`**. Entry checks `Declarator+0x50 != 0` (declarator has content, else jump to the empty path 0xc273fbd), spills `r9` (names vector) to `rbp+0x3258` and `rdx` (out) to `rbp+0x32b8`, and **moves the Declarator's 3-pointer vector out (`Declarator+0x20 begin, +0x28 end, +0x30 cap` read then zeroed)** into `rbp+0x3220/0x3230` - this vector is the list of declared pass/task entries consumed by the generator (so the Declarator owns its entries until generate takes them).
**IntraFrameAllocator**: `[IFA+0x00]` -> pointer to an allocator object `A`, and `[A+0x00]` is the arena/resource handed as `rcx` to the allocation helper **`0xa78940(arena, out{ptr, shared_ptr ctrl}*, size, count)`** (called with size 0x38, count 1 here; used 14 times in the function with `lock dec [ctrl+8]` / `[ctrl+0xc]` release of the temporary control block afterwards, i.e. a shared_ptr-like pool block that lives for the frame). The first block (0x38 bytes) is initialized as an **intrusive list sentinel**: `{+0x00 self, +0x08 self, +0x10 self, +0x18 u16 = 0x0101}` (the container of declared passes for this frame, stored at `rbp+0x3290`). Frees at the end go through `0xe935700(ptr, size)`.
So the IntraFrameAllocator is a thin wrapper over a pooled arena (`A->arena`) with a per-frame lifetime; allocations are `0xa78940` blocks with a refcounted control block, containers built on them start from self-linked sentinels (std::list/ordered map style nodes), consistent with `0x9bc830` being the small-vector push helper (x42 in this function).

### 6cz. 0xc273420 sub-step helpers are vector growth, not pass logic (C7a/C7b) [V static, 62.01]
Re-reading the hot callees: **0xc27f940** is `std::vector<E40>::_Emplace_reallocate` for a **40-byte element** (size via `imul 0xCCCCCCCCCCCCCCCD` = /40, `inc`, cap growth); **0xc27fcc0** is the same for a **24-byte element**; `0x9bc830` is the small-vector push; they are called from 5 sites at 0xc278508/0xc27859e/0xc278625 (40-byte vector at `rbp+0x3330/+0x3338/+0x3340` = begin/end/cap) and 0xc27881d/0xc278882 (24-byte vector at `rbp+0x3348/+0x3350/+0x3358`). Elements are built from a declared entry `d` (`[d+8]` u64, `[d+0x10]` u32, `[d+0x18]`, `[d+0x28]`): the 24-byte element is `{u64 handle/hash, shared_ptr{ptr, ctrl}}` (`lock inc [ctrl+8]`) and the 40-byte one adds `{u32, u16/u8 flags}` after it (`mov [rbp+0x1fe4], 0 / byte [rbp+0x1fd8]=1` are the flag bytes: 1 = bound, 0 = unbound). So these two vectors collect **the per-pass resource dependency records** (buffer/texture handle + owner shared_ptr + usage flags; 40-byte = with explicit state/desc, 24-byte = without): the five Bind* dependency kinds of 6aq all end up in these vectors. `0xc27ec60` (x2 at 0xc277e8c, 0xc2793fe) is the single remaining sub-step, bracketing the per-pass loop (everything between 0xc277e8c and 0xc2793fe, ~0x1a00 bytes) which iterates the moved-out declared vector and fills those records; `0xbc76610` (0xc2797b5), `0x67bf2c0` (0xc27a65a), `0x6759ea0` (0xc27a7c8/0xc27aac2), `0xbc752f0` (0xc27a91e) run after it (view/resource setup and the merge), and `0xc280010` (0xc27ac5f) is the final step producing the task list that endFrame passes to 0xe9002e0.
Take-away for passes: names are in the `names*` vector (r9) plus the entries' hashed names; the resolved order of passes is the vector the final step 0xc280010 produces (Entry 0x80, 6cx), so a live read of that list (kind + HashedString of each entry) is the way to dump the actual frame pass order.

### 6da. LIVE: the per-frame task list at 0xe9002e0 (1.26.62.01, Lua pause-and-read, read-only) [V live]
Breakpoint at `base+0xe9002e0` (task dispatch), `rcx` = taskList: `[rcx]`..`[rcx+8]` = begin/end, **n = 18 entries of 0x80 bytes** in the sampled frame (a world frame on the non-RTX path), `[rcx+0x18]` = 0. Entry layout (consistent across all 18): `+0x00` task object* (its `[0]` = vtable), `+0x08` shared_ptr control block*, `+0x10` pointer (heap, per-task state), `+0x18` u64 small value (0x18, 5, 17, 4, 16, 3 -> **task param/kind index**, 5 for 12 of 18), `+0x20` u64 `0x4BA175A2D6006D58`-like value identical in all entries (a context/frame id or shared hash, not per-task), `+0x28` u32 = 1 (live flag; 0 for entry 13), `+0x2C` u32 = **debug marker colour 0xFFRRGGBB** (0xFF595959, 0xFF151515, 0xFF5A5A5A, 0xFF8E8E8E, 0 for others) used for GPU event ranges, rest zero up to `+0x78` (`+0x78` of the last entry holds three adjacent heap pointers = a small vector).
**Task classes seen (vtable rva, count)**: `0xF25C7B0` x14 (the common pass task; slots: 0x568C240 / 0x568C210 / 0x568BA80 / 0x76920 / 0x123720 / 0x567A080), `0xF25C850` x2 (entries 0 and 13; slots 0x569A160 / 0x568C210 / 0x5699C80 / 0x76920 / 0xA2DA0), `0xF440770` x1 (entry 15: 0xBC38AB0 / 0xBC38A80 / 0xBC38C10 = bgfx-area task, e.g. a blit/present-like task), `0xF43E9C0` x1 (entry 16: 0xBC01E80 / 0xBC01E50 / 0xBC01FE0), `0xF25C080` (entry 17, last: 0x56AECD0 / 0x56AECA0 / 0x56AEE00; heap vector at +0x78 => the final "present"/composition task). All classes share slot1 `0x568C210` (common base: name/destroy) and slot3 `0x76920` (the `+0x18` virtual called by the dispatcher = a trivial getter returning the same kind for all, so the dispatcher's `kind==1` branch is the default path for every task). The pass bodies of the 14 common tasks are therefore slot 0 `0x568C240` (execute) and slot 2 `0x568BA80`; the RTTI of these vtables is not decodable by CE (the COL pointer at vtable[-1] is not a standard MSVC COL here: both common classes point to the same record), so class names have to come from the `0x568C240` body (hash/name constants) instead.
This pins the frame-pass order for a basic world frame: ~14 uniform pass tasks + 2 (first and 14th) of a second class + 3 closing tasks (bgfx-side).

### 6db. LIVE: frame pass names and task class layouts (names read from the task list, 1.26.62.01) [V live + static]
Every task class has virtual slot 2 = `getName()` returning `{char* , len}` of a `std::string` embedded in the object (MSVC SSO, cap at +0x10 of the string < 0x10 -> inline); slot 0 = scalar deleting dtor (the `edx` bit0 = delete, size passed to `0xe935700`); slot 1 (`0x568C210` on the common class) = **invoke the stored callable**: `if ([obj+0x70]) call [[obj+0x70]->vtbl+0x10]([obj+0x70], obj+0x78) else terminate(0xeec59e0)` i.e. the pass body is a type-erased functor at `obj+0x70` (std::function-like, args block at `obj+0x78`); slot 3 `0x76920` returns the kind used by the dispatcher.
| class vtable rva | dtor | task object size | name std::string at | seen as |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `0xF25C7B0` (0x568C240) | 0x568C240 | 0x2E0 | `+0x2C0` | "Scene" (x12), "HUD" |
| `0xF25C850` (0x569A160) | 0x569A160 | 0x? | `+0x88` | "[ClearColorDepth][Scene]", "[ClearDepth][HUD]" (clear-then-draw wrapper tasks) |
| `0xF440770` (0xBC38AB0) | 0xBC38AB0 | - | `+0x290` | "Pass" (bgfx-side generic pass) |
| `0xF43E9C0` (0xBC01E80) | 0xBC01E80 | - | `+0x130` | "TerrainAtlasBlit" |
| `0xF25C080` (0x56AECD0) | 0x56AECD0 | - | `+0x80` | "SceneWaitOnChunk" (last task: waits for chunk-build jobs) |
**Sampled frame order (18 tasks):** `[ClearColorDepth][Scene]`, `Scene` x12, `[ClearDepth][HUD]`, `HUD`, `Pass`, `TerrainAtlasBlit`, `SceneWaitOnChunk`. So the non-RTX frame is: clear colour+depth -> 12 scene sub-tasks (one per view/layer; the same name) -> clear depth -> HUD (UI, the 0x6664a00 path) -> a generic pass -> terrain-atlas blit -> wait-on-chunk sync. Debug colours (6da) per task are in the `+0x2C` of each list entry. This also tells the per-frame hook cost: only ~18 entries to wrap.
Hook use: the dispatch `0xe9002e0` entry list can be walked (begin/end at `[rcx]`,`[rcx+8]`, stride 0x80) from a hook to rename/skip/duplicate tasks; task kind/name read through slot 2.

### 6dc. LIVE: the pass body functors (std::function impls) of the 18 frame tasks [V live + static]
Each task object holds a **std::function** at `obj+0x70` (impl pointer; MSVC `_Func_impl` vtable slots: 0 `_Copy`, 1 `_Move`, 2 `_Do_call`, 3 `_Target_type`, 4/5 delete/dtor). Live functor vtables (rva) per task: task 1 `0xF43FE70`, 2 `0xF43FF00`, 3 `0xF43FF60`, 4 `0xF43FFC0`, 5 `0xF440020`, 6 `0xF440140`, 7 `0xF440200`, 8 `0xF440260`, 9 `0xF25BF90`, 10 `0xF440440`, 11 `0xF440500`, 12 `0xF43FE10`, HUD(14) `0xF4406E0`, Pass(15) `0xF440850`, TerrainAtlasBlit(16) `0xF43E9E0`, SceneWaitOnChunk(17) `0xF43FEA0`, clear tasks `0xF25C9B0` (ClearColorDepth) and `0xF25C9E0` (ClearDepth): these are **distinct lambda types**, so the pass bodies are statically identifiable by their `_Do_call` (slot 2) targets: all 12 Scene functors + HUD share `_Do_call = 0x568CD70` = thunk `rcx=rdx; rdx=r8; jmp 0x568CD90` (the captureless wrapper; the lambda is empty: `_Copy` just stores the static vtable `0xBC36B60`-style), so the **common Scene/HUD task body is 0x568CD90 (0x6F0 bytes)**: it allocates per-task arrays with `0xa78940` (x12), loops with `/24` strides (0xAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAB division = 24-byte dependency records, matching 6cz), calls the resource-binding helpers `0xeec6e40`/`0xeec6de0` (x10/x8), `0x5689800`, `0x56894c0`, `0x568db90`. The different `_Copy` slots (0xBC36B60, 0xBC36C70, 0xBC36CC0, 0xBC36D10, 0xBC36D60, 0xBC36E50, 0xBC36EF0, 0xBC36F40, 0x56AE9C0, 0xBC370D0, 0xBC37170, 0xBC36B10, 0xBC37300) each belong to the per-task lambda that holds the pass's real draw code (they live in the 0xBC3.... region = bgfx-side pass functions: the 12 Scene passes are separate functions `Scene[0..11]` there). For a hook that wants a specific pass: hook the lambda's `_Do_call` thunk or its target after the thunk, or simply swap the `std::function` impl pointer at `task+0x70` (the dispatcher invokes it via slot 1 of the task = `[[obj+0x70]+0x10](obj+0x70, obj+0x78)` => `_Do_call`).

### 6dd. The Scene/HUD task body 0x568CD90 is the bgfx per-view setup; per-view struct at Context+0x1EAB090 [V static, 62.01]
`0x568CD90(a=rcx, task=rdx)` (reached through the std::function thunk 0x568CD70, 6dc): `viewId = u16 [[a+8]+8]`; `ctx = [base+0x12AE1748]` (bgfx::Context*, verified by the rip-relative at 0x568CDD0); `V = ctx + 0x1EAB090 + viewId*0x140` (`shl eax,6; lea eax*5` = *0x140). It copies the task's view description (task object fields) into the **per-view record (0x140 bytes)**:
- `V+0x00` u32 = `pmaxsw([task+0xF8] (i16 x4: x,y,..), 0)` = viewRect.x,y (clamped >= 0); `V+0x04` u32 = `[task+0xFC]` (w,h) with the `pcmpeqw/psubw` fixup (0 -> w,h +1 clamp trick: `x - (x==0 ? -1 : 0)` makes zero extents 1): viewRect size.
- `V+0x10` 0x40-byte matrix <- `[ctx.. +0x1EAB0A0]` source `task+0x?` (first copy src is rdx+`..`, the `0xebce390(dst,src,0x40)` memcpy helper): **view matrix**; `V+0x50` <- `task+0xB8` (0x40): **projection matrix**; `V+0x90` <- `task+0x10C` (0x40) and `V+0xD0` <- `task+0x14C` (0x40): the previous-frame view/projection or view-projection pair (used by TAA/motion); `V+0x110` (0x10 bytes) <- `task+0x18C`: viewport/clear vec4; **`V+0x122` byte <- `task+0x108` = view sort mode (1 Sequence, 2 DepthAsc, 3 DepthDesc)**: exactly the byte the Encoder::submit key builder reads at `viewId*0x140 + 0x1EAB1B2` (6cq) - this ties the two findings; the `ctx+0x1EAAC60 + viewId*4` array (the Sequence counters, `lock xadd` in submit) sits just before.
So **per-view camera matrices live in the bgfx Context at `+0x1EAB0A0 (view) / +0x1EAB0E0 (proj)` + viewId*0x140** and are refreshed by this task each frame; reading them from a hook gives the exact world->view and projection used for the frame without touching ClientInstance. The tail of the function (0x568D480 end) continues with the shared_ptr copies (`task+0x10` control block lock inc) and per-view resource-list/dependency vectors (24-byte records, 6cz) and the `0xa78940` pool allocations.

**6dd live confirmation (CE read-only, 1.26.62.01, world loaded, ctx 0x4BDF6010040):** views 0..6 each show `rect = (0,0,1920,1137)` and sort mode byte `+0x122 = 1` (Sequence). `V+0x10` = identity 4x4 for all of them (the game renders camera-relative, so the *view* matrix is identity and the camera translation goes into per-draw transforms), `V+0x50` = **projection**: `[0.759 0 0 0 | 0 1.28 0 0 | 0 0 -1 -1 | 0 0 -0.025 0]` for views 1..6 (view 0 is identity = the clear/utility view). Check: 1.28/0.759 = 1.686 = 1920/1137 (aspect) so m00 = 1/(aspect*tan(fov/2)), m11 = 1/tan(fov/2) -> **vertical fov = 2*atan(1/1.28) = 76.9 degrees** (the player's FOV setting), m22 = -1, m23 = -1 (column-major, right-handed, w = -z), m32 = -0.025 (= -2*near with infinite far: near = 0.0125?/0.05 under the reverse convention; treat as `-near*...` and verify with a second FOV). So per-view FOV/aspect can be read directly from `ctx+0x1EAB090+viewId*0x140+0x50`.

### 6de. LIVE re-check of 6cq-6cs against a real frame (1.26.62.01, CE read-only) + CORRECTION of 6cs [V live]
Frame pointers swap every frame (`[ctx+0x1CFD0C0]` and `[ctx+0x1CFD0C8]` alternate; the one holding items had **`Frame+0xDC57E8` = 611 items** (the other 0 = already consumed/reset; counters 34278/34279 consecutive). Confirmed in the live header array (0x80 stride at `Frame+0xB4700`): `+0x20` u64 = draw state exactly as 6ct (`0x010000500000001F` default seen 8x, `...27` 12x, `...17` 20x, blend states such as `0x010000500626508F` x162 and `0x010000400000001F` x161 etc.: **17 distinct states among 611 items**), sorted index list at `+0x94700` starts `0,1,2,3,4,5,7,8,10,11,13,6,...` (stable ascending with view/key grouping), sort keys at `+0x14700` with **viewId = key>>54: v6=166, v11=132, v12=132, v16=96, v15=37, v5=10, v3=9, v2=8, v10=5 ... (17 views used; same view ids as the per-view records of 6dd)**. Header fields seen: `+0x00` u32 (resource-set/bind index, e.g. 0x17174, 0xBE54, 0xB740), `+0x04` u16 x2 (e.g. 4,0x0D / 5,5 / 4,9: stream/layout handles), `+0x0C` u32 = 0 for all 611 (index-buffer handle 0; non-indexed = 0xFFFF would show as FFFF - none: all draws indexed), `+0x34/+0x38/+0x3C` = (startIndex 0x298, ?0x3AC, count 0x31 = 49 indices etc.), `+0x40` u32 (vertex offset e.g. 0xF57B), **`+0x44` = 0x54 (vertex stride 84, the same 0x54 layout record stride the executor multiplies in 0xe822087)**, `+0x48` 2/4 (stream count/instances), `+0x5C` = 0x0001FFFF.
**Correction to 6cs:** byte `h+0x23` is NOT an item-kind byte for these headers: it is bits 24-31 of the draw state (blend dst-alpha factor and equation), live values 0 (x306), 6 (x302), 1 (x1), 2 (x2). The executor's `cmp byte [r8+0x23],0 / ==1` therefore tests something else, or `[rbx+0x590]` is not the `Frame+0xB4700` array but a second 0x80-stride table (e.g. the "draw-header2"/dispatch descriptors); the kind-1 special call (vtbl +0x200) must be re-derived with a breakpoint on 0xe8222d4-area (62.01) before being trusted. The indexed vs non-indexed rule via `h+0x0C` (0xFFFF = none) is consistent with the live data (`+0x0C` = 0 -> indexed).
Net: 6cq (encoder), 6ct/6cu (state/enums), 6cw (RVAs) are live-consistent; 6cs's header-kind semantics are withdrawn.

**6de follow-up (static, 62.01):** `[rbx+0x590] = Frame + 0xB4700` is confirmed (`lea r8,[rax+0xb4700]` at 0xe821853 in the executor setup; `[rbx+0x588] = ctx+0x9B2178` is the index-buffer record table (0x80 stride), `[rbx+0x5E0] = ctx+0xB62178` the program/layout table, `[rbx+0x628] = ctx+0xC97698`), so `h` IS the live header and the test is really on state bits 24-31. In 62.01 the checks are at `0xe8223cd` (`cmp byte [h+0x23],0 ; je 0xe822480`) and `0xe829b7f` (`cmp byte [h+0x23],1`): **byte3 == 0 -> branch A at 0xe822480 (taken by the 306 live items with no blend dst-alpha/equation bits), byte3 != 0 -> branch B at 0xe829b50 (302 live items with 0x06, and the 3 with 1/2), inside which byte3 == 1 takes the extra-args call (cmdlist vtbl +0x200)**. So "kind" in 6cs is a state-derived selector, not a separate field; both branches contain draw emission (branch B was the one decoded in 6cs). Branch A (0xe822480) is not decoded yet; do that next to finish B3c.

### 6df. Executor branch A (0xe822480) = program/shader variant resolve [V static, 62.01]
Branch A starts with `r15` (the per-item 64-bit value loaded earlier in the item loop; its bits 21..31 hold the **program index**): `prog = (r15>>0x15) & 0x7FF`, `rec = [rbx+0x5E0] + prog*0xF8` (`rbx+0x5E0 = ctx+0xB62178` = **program table, stride 0xF8** - the same `imul 0xF8 ; add 0xB62178` the PSO builder 0xe81e860 starts with, 6ct). `esi = u32 [[rec]+0xA8]` = the program's **variant key**; it is hashed bytewise (`movsx` of each of the 4 bytes, `*0x1003F + next`, final `* 0xFC5D1543EC5F01`, `& (bucketCount-2)`) into the shader-variant hash map in `[[rbx+0xA0]+0x48..+0x50]` (buckets of 16-byte {begin,end} pairs, chain nodes `{key u32 @+0, next @+0x10}`); hit -> jump `0xe822a40` (use cached variant), miss -> zero `[rbx+0x220]`, build a 0x38-byte request (`0xebce430` memset, `[rbx+0x3b0]=[[rbx+0xa0]+0x1248]` (device), `[rbx+0x3b8]` = shader blob/program pointer chain `[[[rec]]]`, size from `+8`) and create the variant. So branch A (items without blend high bits) and branch B both end in the same draw emission, branch A just resolves the program variant first (branch B items have it resolved by the PSO builder path via `0xe81e860`, which takes the state and the program record). The program record layout (known): `+0x00` ptr to a shader-set object whose `[0]` -> `[0]` is the compiled blob pointer, `+0xA8` u32 variant key; table index = program handle (the Encoder `program` u16 from 6cq, 11 bits like the key field).

### 6dg. F5c recipe: enable / disable / reorder frame passes (design from 6cx, 6da-6dc; untested in game)
Hook point: **`0xe9002e0(taskList* rcx, args* rdx)`** (62.01, not shifted; frame-graph task dispatch, called once per frame from `BgfxContext::endFrame` 0xc270460 with the list produced by 0xc273420). On entry:
1. `begin=[rcx]`, `end=[rcx+8]`, entries are 0x80 bytes (6cx/6da). For each entry `e`: `obj=[e]`, name = `{char*,len}` from virtual `obj->vtbl[2]` (`getName`, 6db) (std::string inline, SSO when `cap < 0x10`); known names this frame: `[ClearColorDepth][Scene]`, `Scene` x12, `[ClearDepth][HUD]`, `HUD`, `Pass`, `TerrainAtlasBlit`, `SceneWaitOnChunk` (RTX/VV frames have other names; the declarations are in 0x55f1450 / 0xbbe2d50).
2. **Disable a pass:** keep the entry but make its callable a no-op: `std::function` impl at `[obj+0x70]` (MSVC `_Func_impl`, `_Do_call` = vtable slot 2) -> point `[obj+0x70]` at a static stub impl whose vtable has slots {copy, move, `_Do_call = ret`, type, delete, dtor}; the dispatcher invokes `[[obj+0x70]->vtbl+0x10](obj+0x70, obj+0x78)`. This keeps list size, view setup ordering and the dependency vectors intact. Do NOT disable the Scene/HUD per-view setup body (0x568CD90, 6dd) unless the whole view is also dropped, because it writes the per-view rect/matrices/sort mode that submit reads.
3. **Reorder:** swap two raw 0x80-byte entries in place (ownership = `{obj, ctrl}` pair, the move is a plain memcpy so refcounts stay consistent); respect dependencies: dependency vectors were built from declaration order (0xc273420), so only reorder tasks with no resource dependency between them (e.g. HUD tasks relative to TerrainAtlasBlit) or after the dispatch `std::vector<u16>` run-order (built at the top of 0xe9002e0, `rbp+0x110`) is considered.
4. **Remove:** `memmove` later entries down and `end -= 0x80` (leaks one shared_ptr ref and one task object per frame, acceptable for a debug hook; the proper way is calling the entry's dtor: `obj->vtbl[0](obj, 1)` for kind-1 tasks only after dropping the control block ref).
5. **Add a pass:** not supported by this hook (new tasks must be declared in 0xc273420 inputs); use `Encoder::submit` (0xbc60740) for extra draws instead.
Detection of the active renderer: first entry names (`[ClearColorDepth][Scene]` = Fancy path above); VV/RTX names come from the declaration tables (6p).

### 6dh. B3d: statistics / timers / debug text inside submit slot 50 (0xe82e230) [V static strings, 62.01]
The D3D12 `RendererContext::submit` (slot 50) contains the **stock bgfx debug overlay and timing code inlined** (strings between 0xe831f8b and 0xe832636): ` %s (FL %d.%d) / Clang 22.1.5 / x86 / 64-bit / Windows` (device + feature level, the exe is built with Clang 22.1.5), ` Device: %s`, ` Memory: %s (video), %s (system), %s (shared), %s (process)` with per-heap lines `Local` / `Non-local` (`%s - Budget: %10s, Usage: %10s, AvailRes: %10s, CurrRes: %10s`: DXGI QueryVideoMemoryInfo), `Frame: min,max`, `Present: min,max`, ` Reset flags: [%c] vsync, [%c] MSAAx%d, [%c] MaxAnisotropy`, ` Submitted: %5d (draw %5d, compute %4d) / CPU %7.4f [ms]`, per-primitive stats `Triangles / TriStrip / Lines / LineStrip / Points: %7d (#inst: %5d), submitted: %7d`, ` [F11 - RenderDoc capture]`, `Indices: %7d`, `DVB size`, `DIB size` (dynamic vertex/index buffer sizes), `Capture: %7.4f [ms]`, `Submit wait: %7.4f [ms]`, `Render wait: %7.4f [ms]`. These are drawn by the bgfx debug-text path only when the debug flag BGFX_DEBUG_STATS/TEXT is set (flag test near 0xe831811..0xe831876, `cmp [ctx+0x3c5640c], 0x7fffffff` sentinel is the debug-text row counter); the per-frame **timers** that feed them are the bgfx `Stats` block (CPU timer begin/end, GPU timestamp queries ("TI3" = timer index) through the `[rip+0x4382d36..0x4382c29]` globals written at 0xe831c33-0xe831d5d: submit/render wait timestamps, frame min/max). The `m_cmd/d3d12` loop emission itself (draw/dispatch calls through the command list wrapper at `[rbx+0x90]+0x30`) carries no marker strings (no PIX/`setMarker` text in the executor; GPU debug events are produced by the task layer: the entry colour at `+0x2C` in 6da).
Hook use: turning on the debug stats overlay is a flag write in the context (debug flags field next to `ctx+0x3c5640c`'s sibling); the stats counters (`Submitted`, per-primitive) can be read from the globals above instead of drawing the overlay. This completes B3d at structural level; flag bit positions to confirm live if needed.

### 6di. A11 consolidated: init / shutdown lifecycle (from 6ak, 6c, 6al, 6dh) [V static]
Order, game side -> bgfx -> D3D12: (1) game init function `0x4661780` calls `Rendering_BgfxInit` (string anchor 0x466de7c), `Rendering_BgfxStartFrame` (0x466e17c) and `Rendering_GraphicsTasksInit` (0x466ea7c) (6j); (2) `bgfx::Context::init` core **0xbc63690** (state machine: `0xbc634b0`/`0xbc6d750` check "InitBegin has not been called", "Already initialized", "Renderer not initialized, but InitState is in End!"; async variant `initAsync`): spawns the **"bgfx - renderer backend thread"** (proc `0xbc65720` -> render-frame step `0xbc63150`), initialises the clear-colour palette and the view array (stride 0x140, i.e. the per-view records of 6dd at `Context+0x1EAB090`) and the encoder/frame arrays; (3) the renderer backend object is created through the context vtable: **slot 58 (+0x1D0) `0xe807ff0` = device init** (kernel32 `CreateEventExA`, d3d12.dll `D3D12CreateDevice`, `D3D12EnableExperimentalFeatures`, `D3D12GetDebugInterface`, `D3D12SerializeRootSignature`; RTX class adds the DLSS gate in slot 64 `0xe8403d0`: `SuperSampling.NeedsUpdatedDriver / MinDriverVersionMajor / MinDriverVersionMinor / Available`); (4) first frame: `Rendering_BgfxStartFrame` -> `BgfxFrameBuilder` per-frame; (5) **device lost / TDR** = slot 7 `0xe8102b0` (0x1924 bytes; "Failed to create resource", "Invalid Shader", "Unable Initialize"); `_initiateTDR` stub (frame-builder slot 113 `0x768f0`); (6) **shutdown/reset = slot 59 `0xe80dec0`** (releases the 0x19F08 block: command lists/fences), destructor slot 0 (`0xe83c630` base, `0xe84b280` RTX). `bgfx::reset` (resolution/vsync/MSAA flags: the overlay line "Reset flags: [vsync] [MSAAx%d] [MaxAnisotropy]" of 6dh) is processed on the render thread at the top of `submit`; its flag word is the same one printed there.
Open for A11: the exact reset entry (flags -> swapchain resize) and the shutdown ordering; both are only reachable by a breakpoint on slot 59 during window resize / exit (not done to avoid destabilizing the game).

**6de/6cs CORRECTION 2 (field names):** the already-verified `src/sdk/mc/deps/bgfx/Frame.h` `RenderDrawHeader` is right and the guesses in 6cs/6de were wrong: header `+0x40` = startIndex, **`+0x44` = numIndices (live 0x54 = 84 for those draws, NOT a vertex stride)**, `+0x48` = index stride (2/4), `+0x4C` = numVertices, `+0x50` vertex byte offset, `+0x54` vertex count, `+0x58` u16 vertex stride, `+0x5A` u16 index buffer handle, `+0x76` u16 vertex buffer handle. The `h+0x0C` index-handle and DrawInstanced-argument readings of 6cs belong to the *other* record the executor builds (the per-draw record at `[Frame+0x8B4700]+idx*0x540`, offsets +0x0C/+0x10..+0x18 of THAT record), not to the 0x80 header. The state-derived branch test (`byte [h+0x23]`, 6de follow-up, 6df) stands.
