### 6io. LIVE: sort keys and the sorted list of a 1106-item frame (62.01, populated server) [V live, I meanings]
`F + 0x14700` = u64 sort key per item, `F + 0x94700` = u16 per sorted slot (render item id; 4 ids per qword as seen in a raw dump), n = `u32 [F+0xDC57E8]`. Decoded over the whole frame: every key has bit 52 set (draw), bit 53 clear (no custom items in this scene), bits 50..51 = 2 on all 1106 items (the "sequence" draw type of 6fg), view field = bits 54..61 holding 0..15 with a populated mix (view 2: 326 items, 6: 134, 11/12: 119 each, 10: 84, 3: 89, 5: 80, 15: 37, 4: 12, others 1-2). Inside a view the low bits carry a per-item counter and the program: consecutive items show low32 = 0x30000000, 0x70000000 (step 0x40000000), 0x01560000, 0x80BC0000, 0xC0BC0000, then the counter carries into bit 32 (0x0098_0001_00BC0000): a counter in bits 30..~49 stepping by 1 per submitted item and a program/state field in bits 16..29 (0x156, 0xBC...) [I]. The sorted list follows key order with 10 exceptions in this frame (for example slot 329 holds id 3 whose key is smaller than the key of the previous slot): the keys at +0x14700 are rewritten by Frame::sort's view remap AFTER ordering, so comparing the stored keys with the slot order is not valid without applying the remap [I: not reproduced]. First slots: ids 0, 1, 2, 5, 6, 8 (views 0, 0, 1, 2, 2, 2). Consequence for hooks: a veto/reorder pass must work on the slot list (u16 ids) and on the header via id, not on the stored keys.

## 8. Replacement Architecture

Prescriptive model for replacing pieces of the frame pipeline, written against the L1-L18 layer model (section 0) and the reverse-verified seams in section 6. Nothing here assumes patch dates or timing.

Ground rules (carried from L18/M5):

- Static analysis, plain-memory polling, and offline snapshots only. No debugger attach or breakpoints; no patches without an explicit go-ahead.
- Every hook target is validated at startup: the slot/sig must point into .text, the signature must be found, and content checks (mask immediates, offset references) must pass. Fail loudly otherwise.
- All version-specific addresses live in OffsetProvider.h / Sigs.json; behavior anchors are strings, `__FUNCSIG__` substrings, or FNV-1 64 name hashes so they survive re-mapping.
- Claims are tagged **[V]** (verified in disassembly or live), **[I]** (inferred), **[?]** (open).

### 8a. Replacement seams (what can be replaced)

1. **Submit takeover (0xe82e230; entered only through the RTX override 0xe83d440, slot 50 of the live vtable 0xF4EEE80 [V 6al]).** RendererContext::submit walks the Frame and records D3D12 (slot 50, submit stats/timers inline) [section 0; slot per K-P5]. A replacement submit can walk the same Frame layout, reuse the PSO builder (0xe81dc60) and the descriptor/upload rings, and issue its own draws. Frame contract for a replacement walk (from the submit example in section 0):

   - draw records at Frame+0xB4700, stride 0x80 [V stride; base corrected to 0xB4700 in 6am]
   - draw index allocated by CAS at frame+0xDC57E8 (u32 m_numRenderItems), cap 0xFFFE [V per Frame.h]; the RenderDraw array is at +0x8B4700, the 0x80-byte headers at +0xB4700 (audit 6ii)
   - per-view block table: [frame+0xDC57E0][viewSlot] [I]
   - backbuffer size: frame+0xDC5808 {u32 width, u32 height} = 1920x1137 [V]
   - Frame counter: frame+0xE746D8 u32 [V]
   - renderer state: this+0x19F58 resource tables -> guarded paged heap arrays [I]; this+0x19F88 int (<5 = skip) [I]; per-handle records at this+0x93A178, array of 0x78-byte records [I]

   Per-draw fields read by the example: program +0x38 [I], startIdx +0x40 [I], indexCnt +0x44 [I] (0xFFFFFFFF = computed = buffer size / index stride [V]), ibHandle +0x5A [V], vbHandle +0x76 [V] (0xFFFF = none), state word d[+0x20] u64 -> PSO key mask 0x0707007FFFFFF0FF [V], bind slots d[+0x63] bitmask of 8 populated slots, slot k at d+k*8 (+4 u16 handle, +6 u16 flags) [V].

2. **Per-draw veto/rewrite at the executor flush.** Draw executor 0xe821120; the batched DrawBatcher flush is 0xe81b720 [6ej] [V]. Read/modify every draw record before the GPU; needs the header-to-record mapping and an in-game test [M3e].

3. **Custom render item.** Encoder::submitCustom(view, fn, user) with key bit 53: the executor calls fn(user, ctx) on the render thread in view order with the command list [M3f]. Untested in game; ctx layout partly inferred [?].

4. **Pass control.** Enable/disable/reorder whole passes via TaskDispatchHook + dragon/Tasks.h (recipe 6dg/6dy) [M3c]. Written, never built or run.

5. **Whole-frame takeover.** Drive the frame ourselves from a frame-level substitute for submit (L18 goal; runtime needs listed in section 7 "Open items").

### 8b. Frame double buffering and semaphores

Two Frame instances are spaced 0xE749C0 apart. The render thread signals ctx+0x80 (frame-ready semaphore) and waits ctx+0x100 (frame-consumed semaphore) [A10; not verified in this binary: [?]]. A replacement submit must preserve the same signal/wait order or the game thread and render thread deadlock [I].

### 8c. State a replacement must preserve

- PSO builder 0xe81dc60: decodes draw state to a D3D12 PSO desc, key mask known [B4]. Replacement draws route through it or rebuild an equivalent key.
- Per-view setup 0xe818490 (called three times inside submit [6t]); frame begin, descriptor/upload ring begin, clear/RT transitions surround the per-draw consumer [section 0 example].
- Disk cache 0xe8feb10: shader/texture blobs are served from it; a replacement must consume or invalidate it consistently [B4].
- D3D12 65-slot interface [6f; K-P5]: slot 6 Present, slot 60 reset, slot 59 shutdown, slot 7 TDR, slot 50 submit stats/timers, slot 26 CreateTexture, slot 22 CreateShader, slot 24 CreateProgram, slots 14/16/17 vertex/index buffer, slot 35 CreateFrameBuffer, slots 36-38 uniform, slot 30 readTexture.

### 8d. Version anchoring

- Pass/material name hashes: FNV-1 64, basis 0xcbf29ce484222325, prime 0x100000001b3 [V per 6o/6p; K-P2].
- String-based discovery via srcloc.py (__FUNCSIG__ substring) survives RVA moves [K-P8].
- Per-version RVA/slot database: OffsetProvider.h + Sigs.json [K-P7]; new sigs recorded beside each function's RVA with a behavior paragraph so they survive a patch [M4b/M4c].

## 9. Build Capability Spec

What the replacement module must build, and how correctness is gated before anything runs in game.

### 9a. Artifacts

- One DLL module exporting init/shutdown and the seam entry points (submit replacement, pass toggles, draw veto).
- SDK headers: Context/Frame/RenderItem/View/Encoder/Handles (bgfx core), d3d12/*.h (D3D12 backend), dragon/*.h (RenderDragon), one header per description (FrameBuilder layer), plus Descriptions.h and FrameBuilder.h. These headers are part of the client build now (Release builds OK through 6ig); not exercised in game [6ii].
- OffsetProvider.h + Sigs.json: the per-version RVA/slot database used to re-map on patch [K-P7].
- layout_asserts.json + tools/sigs/layoutcheck.py (168 assertions pass on 62.01 as of 6ia; 55 was the older count) and RenderLayoutAsserts.h static_asserts, run from the SelfTest world phase (Release build OK; not run in game) [M4c]. Every newly reversed function adds its constants to both lists.

### 9b. Startup validation (fail loudly)

- Each hooked slot must point into .text; each sig must be found; content checks (mask immediates, offset references) must pass [K-P9; L18b: content checks to code in SelfTest, not built].
- SelfTest validates sizes and offsets that can be validated at startup: static_asserts + SelfTest [M3a].

### 9c. Build gating

- Release build of the client (SelfTest and the render headers included) passes; none of it has run in game for the render rewrite. No in-game patches without an explicit go-ahead [M5].
- Port check: re-run the dossier tool on previous versions (BS_EXE) for every D2 function to measure what moved (RVA, offsets, slots); not yet re-run on older exes [M4d; sigs audited on 50.04/51.01/60.05/62.01 at BGFX_MAP 6ey].

## 10. Integration Points

Existing surfaces the replacement touches, with status.

- 0xe82e230 submit (RendererContextD3D12, slot 50 stats/timers): hook target for submit takeover; submit takes Frame* in rdx [I per K-P6].
- 0xe81dc60 PSO builder: decodes draw state to a D3D12 PSO desc [B4].
- 0xe821120 draw executor: per-draw consumer; batched flush at 0xe81b720 [6ej] [V].
- 0xe818490 per-view setup: called inside submit [6t] [I].
- 0xe8feb10 disk cache: shader/texture blob cache [B4] — pending integration.
- 0x66d9af0 BatchActor extractor: actor-to-draw source; D1+ dossier [6u].
- 0x55f1450 VV giant function (VibrantVisualsFrameRenderer builder) [L6] [I].
- Hooks written but never built or run in game: TaskDispatchHook + dragon/Tasks.h (pass add/remove/reorder) [M3c]; RenderMarkHook (actor-to-draw route) [M3d]; FrameFilterHook (drops inserts by kind; nametags ~35/frame, UI meshes ~25/frame per 6i).
- Encoder::submitCustom (key bit 53) custom-item seam: fn(user, ctx) on the render thread in view order; untested in game, ctx layout partly inferred [M3f] [?].
- Version-stable anchors: FNV-1 64 pass/material name hashes (basis 0xcbf29ce484222325, prime 0x100000001b3); __FUNCSIG__ substrings via srcloc.py [6o/6p; K-P2/K-P8].

## 11. DLL / Module Structure

### 11a. Module layout

- NOT a new DLL (audit 6ii): the replacement is a set of modules inside the existing BlueySense client DLL (src/client/features/render/*, hooks under src/client/features/hooks/hooks/render/). It is built and SelfTest runs from the world phase. Seams are registered through the rewrite registry of section 12 (R1), not exported entry points.
- Shutdown mirrors slot 59 ordering; reset entry is the submit prologue plus slot 60 [A11] — the DLL must not break device-lost/TDR handling (slot 7) [B8b].

### 11b. Header ownership

- bgfx core: Context/Frame/RenderItem/View/Encoder/Handles SDK headers [L1].
- D3D12 backend: d3d12/*.h with offsets in OffsetProvider.h [L2; B11 header D3D12Context.h compile-checked, not in DLL build].
- RenderDragon: dragon/*.h [L3; C1 endFrame flow 0xc270460].
- FrameBuilder layer: one header per description [L4]; FrameBuilder.h [6ic]; Descriptions.h with all description types [M2c].
- Headers compile-checked with MSVC /Zs, not built into the DLL yet [6eg].

### 11c. Versioning

- OffsetProvider.h + Sigs.json hold per-version RVA/slot data; re-map on patch by matching sigs, __FUNCSIG__ substrings, and FNV-1 64 name hashes [K-P7/K-P2/K-P8].
- Behavior notes without RVAs (docs/BEHAVIOR_NOTES.md, one paragraph per structural function) survive re-mapping [M4b]; not yet written for M1d/M1e items.

### 11d. Threading contract

- Thread model: game thread, dragon task threads, bgfx render thread [L17e; 6as/6ac]. The replacement's submit runs on the render thread.
- Frame double buffer: two Frame instances 0xE749C0 apart; ctx+0x80 frame-ready semaphore, ctx+0x100 frame-consumed semaphore [A10]. Preserve signal/wait order.
- Custom items via submitCustom are invoked on the render thread in view order [M3f] [?].

## 12. Rewrite Program (audit 6ii; supersedes the seam-only framing of sections 8-11) [I plan, V where cited]

Audit of sections 8-11 and checklist N/O/P (opencode draft), checked against Frame.h, 6al, 6fg/6fh, functions.tsv and the build:
- Right: the seam list (submit 0xe82e230, executor 0xe821120 / flush 0xe81b720, custom item bit 53, TaskDispatch pass control), FNV-1 64 for pass/material names (6p), the startup-validation rule, the no-debugger rule.
- Wrong or stale, fixed in place above: draw cap 0xFFFF (it is 0xFFFE); "single DLL with exported entry points" (the code lives in the client DLL, already built); "headers not built / 55 assertions" (they build; 168 assertions); submit is reached through the RTX override 0xe83d440; the semaphore claims are unverified [?]. N3c "custom items run on the render thread" is [V] by 6fg, not [?].
- Missing (the real gap): sections 8-11 say where to intercept. They say nothing about REWRITING (reimplementing a function so the original is not run), nothing about proving a rewrite equals the original, nothing about measuring that it is faster, and the O/P checklists have no order, dependencies or done-criteria. Section 12 and checklist Q add them.

### 12a. Definition of a rewrite
A rewrite of function F is done when ALL hold: (1) F's dossier has a reversed-source block (M5); (2) our F_new has the same signature, observable effects (memory writes, calls to other game functions, their order) and threading contract; (3) shadow-mode comparison against the original over real frames shows zero divergences (12b R2); (4) a measured win, or an explicit reason (control, tracing), is recorded; (5) a registry entry (R1) switches F between original and rewrite at runtime, with a version gate; (6) all layout constants are in both assertion lists. Fail fast: a mismatch in verify mode aborts loudly, no silent fallback guard (project rule).

### 12b. Infrastructure to build before the first rewrite
- R1 Rewrite registry: {name, sig-resolved rva, original trampoline, replacement, mode off / shadow / on / verify}. Installed through the existing detour layer; the original stays callable through the trampoline.
- R2 Shadow/verify harness: run original and F_new on copies of the same inputs, compare outputs (memory ranges, return value, emitted calls). Pure/leaf functions first. For GPU-facing code compare emitted draw records and bgfx command bytes, not pixels.
- R3 Frame probe ("read anything"): per-frame dump keyed by frame counter of every inserted description (typed fields), the pass list, Frame draw records + RenderDraw + resolved material/program/PSO key, actor attribution (RenderMark), resource tables. Read-only (static + memory polling).
- R4 Profiler: CPU scope timers around registry functions and frame stages; GPU timestamps via a custom item (bit 53) on the render thread. No optimization is accepted without numbers.
- R5 Ownership table per rewritten function: allocator, thread, lifetime (descriptions, the 0xE749C0 double-buffered Frame, command lists).

### 12c. Rewrite order (leaf to root, highest cost first)
1. Pure hot-path helpers, all fully reversed [V]: 0x5679db0 uniform/component writer, 0x675e440 ResourceCache::get (hash + lookup per item: cache per material), 0xc280010/0xc280ca0 sort, matrix-cache setTransform, 0xbc60740 Encoder::submit, discard / RenderBind grow / side-table copy.
2. The 21 thin insert clones (one template) and group visitors become one table-driven inserter; extractors by cost: BatchActor 0x66d9af0, chunk, nametag, particle, sky (needs M1d reads and M2c descriptions).
3. Executor 0xe821120 / flush 0xe81b720: batching and instancing merge, PSO key cache, descriptor build (needs the executor holes closed: bind-cache miss, PSO desc build on a miss, setPredefined, helper bodies).
4. Pass bodies (M1e): replace or reorder passes; our own passes through custom items, in/out resources known.
5. Frame graph generate 0xc273420 and whole-frame takeover; then the optimization module (culling, batching, own upscale).

### 12d. What still must be understood before steps 3-5 are safe
Executor job model (per-job command lists and order), RTX subclass overrides, texture view block +0x20..+0x97, custom-item ctx (0x18 bytes, first field), pass in/out resources, the remaining 45 descriptions, lifetime/freeing of description memory, descriptor-heap and upload-ring ownership, the semaphore handshake (verify live), the device-lost/TDR path.

## 6el. Sky visitor 0x66560f0: opencode decodes folded in (static, 62.01; partial, merge agent)
Details: docs/functions/66560f0_sky_visitor.md; header src/sdk/mc/deps/minecraft_renderer/framebuilder/SkyDescriptions.h (read-views with static_assert offsets, one per quoted read).
Coverage measured by comparing every exe instruction in a range against the text-normalised quotes of the unit files (units U-SK1..U-SK3c): Cubemap shared body 0x6659615..0x665aee8 1189/1194 (the 5 gaps are filled in the dossier), LegacyCubemap/EndSky resolve 0x6656b2b..0x6656d4d 110/110, SkyAmbientSamples 0x665708b..0x665803c 833/836, VolumetricFog 0x66569b1..0x6657e4a 1031/1031, Stars joins 0x665b624..0x665cf64 1402/1402, cold range 0x665cf64..0x665d7e5 412/412.
Facts closed [V rdis]: (1) all sky cases push into the pass object `0x5ABA5C0(0x9D0 + [frameCtx+0x10])` and leave through `jmp 0x665D124`; (2) Cubemap head 0x66562d5 reads `[rdi]` and fields +0x10 (lock inc word) and +0x12 (bit 0) of the pointee, not of the description (this corrects the earlier "desc+0x10/+0x12" note in docs/functions/66560f0.md); mode select `cmp dword [rsi+0x70],1` -> "Cubemap"/"CubemapHDRi", `call 0x67D6D70`, ready flag `cmp byte [r15+0x10],1`, then `je 0x6659615` (0x6656441); Cubemap block copy = desc+0x10..+0x4F (4 vec4, guard byte +0x52==1) and u16 +0x50 (0x665a220..0x665a261); (3) LegacyCubemap reads bytes +0x3E/+0x3F, u16 +0x3C, qword +0x18 which is a POINTER to four vec4 (0x66599be), and +0x30/+0x38 (0x6659b9b/0x6659ba0); (4) SkyAmbientSamples reads u16 +0x00, +0x08, +0x10 (shared ptr + ctrl, lock inc [ctrl+8]); (5) VolumetricFog copies desc +0x00 u16, +0x04..+0x53 (5 vec4), +0x54 qword into a 0x58-byte record at pass+0x158 (stride 0x58, 0x6656adf) or flows into the SkyAmbientSamples body; (6) pass-record pools touched by the sky tails: +0x008 (0xC8 records), +0x150 (0x58), +0x540 (0x130), +0x648 (push 0x675FB30), +0x750 (0x58), +0x768 (0x88, `add [rsi+0x770],0x88` 0x665bd4a), +0x780 (push 0x675F3B0), +0x858 (byte counter), +0x8A0 (u16 array).
UPDATE (static RE pass): Stars, Sky (tag 5), CelestialBody and EndSky bodies, the Atmosphere body, Clouds/Weather tail targets and the payload tails are decoded in docs/functions/66560f0_sky_cases.md. Full decode: docs/functions/6640d80_light_visitor.md; listings docs/data/listings/light_visitor_6640d80_6645400.txt, light_clone_53599d0_535ce80.txt, light_shadowatlas_builder_7915d50_7915fc0.txt; typed read-views `framebuilder/LightDescriptions.h` (static_assert offsets).

So tag 7 is NOT VoxelVolume (6ih guess), tag 6 is.

Builder 0x7915D50 writes four identity rows then a matrix from 0x7912420 and the pair [system+0x178]/[system+0x180] (the atlas texture shared pointer).

- TransitioningVoxelVolume: same +0x00/+0x08/+0x10, +0x18 qword, vector of 8-byte elements +0x20/+0x28, dword array +0x38/+0x40; split into the same VoxelRecord (+0x138 vector) and a 0x20-byte TransitionRecord {qword, vector<u32>} in pass vector +0x168/+0x170/+0x178; DrawItem: qword +0x08 = 0xA000, words +0xAA (transition index) and +0xAE.
- All three leave through the shared epilogue 0x66447a0 after one 0xC8-byte DrawItem append to pass +0x08/+0x10 (0xEEC6EC0 / grow 0x5ABA6D0). Builders: VoxelVolume 0x5AE5760 (optional, engaged byte at desc+0x30), Transitioning 0x6B19E30 (calls 0x5AE5760 for the system's +0x80 manager); internals beyond the first fields and the consumers of the new pass vectors are open [?].

### 6iq. LIVE description dumps via one Lua pause-and-read handler (62.01, 2026-11-15, populated world) [V live]
Method (2 CE calls, no code written, breakpoints removed, game kept running): `debug_process(1)` once, then an `evaluate_lua` that defines `debugger_onBreakpoint` (reads the description, writes docs/data/*.txt with io.open, removes its own breakpoints after 5-25 s, `debug_continueFromBreakpoint(co_run)`). NOTE: `get_breakpoint_hits` registers are truncated to 32 bits for stack addresses (R8 = 0x0523F6D0), so never use logged registers to dereference; read R8 inside the Lua handler instead.
- docs/data/live_chunk_desc_1.26.62.01.txt: 6 distinct ChunkGeometry descriptions (r8 at 0x66ea3d0; pass kinds K = 0, 7, 8, 10 seen, flag50 0/1). Confirmed against ChunkGeometryDescription.h: three make_shared pairs at +0x00/+0x10/+0x20 (second qword = first - 0x10: control block precedes the object), +0x30 = 3, {count,ptr} vector view at +0x38/+0x40 (counts 0x10..0x67), +0x48 = pointer into .data holding K (0xF0A4A20 etc), +0x50 byte flag, FogColor (0.0403, 0.0494, 0.09, 1.0) at +0x54, FogAndDistance (0.656, 1.0, 128.0, 57.0) at +0x64, time 348.04 at +0x74. Open: the header calls +0xB8 and +0xD8 byte flags, but live they are the low bytes of aligned pointers (0x...300 / 0x...000), so they read 0 always; those two meanings are [?] until a non-zero case is found.
- docs/data/live_desc_sky_light_effect_1.26.62.01.txt: 0x300-byte descriptions for visitor/tag pairs sky 0,5,6,7; light 0; effect 1,2,3,4. Sky tag 0 (Atmosphere): vec4 colours (r,g,b,1.0) at +0x24, +0x34, +0x44, +0x54, +0x64, +0x74 (values 0.02/0.2,0.0787,0.0835/0.018,0.0106,0.028/0.1/0.1/0.0402,0.0494,0.09), the last equal to the chunk FogColor, then floats 84, 128, 128, 57 at +0x84..+0x90 (the chunk FogAndDistance uses 128/57 too): fog/atmosphere colour timeline sampled at the current time [V live, meaning I].

- Live (6ir, 2026-11-15): docs/data/live_desc_item_ui_editor_sky2_1.26.62.01.txt = one 0x300-byte description per (visitor, tag) seen in a 30 s window; item/ui/editor/sky visitors.
